This is because he knows how to utilize the material energy for the service of the Lord. In the government, the criminal department and civil department may appear different in the eyes of the citizens, but in the eyes of the government both departments are one and the same. The criminal department is troublesome for the criminal but not for the obedient citizen. Similarly, this material energy is troublesome for the conditioned soul, but it has nothing to do with the liberated souls who are engaged in the service of the Lord.

Thus I conclude that because you were engaged cent percent in the service of the Lord and expertly teaching your followers how to serve the Lord, not only were you liberated but you exhibited real independence from the material energy by taking resolute shelter of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. This is the foundation of your house, in which the whole world can reside. Only small-minded people create disturbances. While looking at your magnanimity, I realize that the dogs keep barking but the elephant walks on. I am enthused to serve your mission because your servants have adopted this attitude. My sacrifice becomes enthusiastic when I remember your magnanimity. Please let it be my source of devotional service forever, or at least for next ten thousand years.

Your enthusiastic servant,  
Vedavyāsapriya Swami

## Vyāsa-Pūjā Homages from ISKCON Centers

## Adelaide

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

“He reasons ill who tells that Vaiṣṇavas die,  
When thou art living still in sound!  
The Vaiṣṇavas die to live, and living try  
To spread the holy name around!”

I reason ill to think that you have left  
Because without your personal form I am bereft.  
To have your darshan one more time  
Would be sweet ambrosia most sublime.

Those joyous days of many years past,  
When you first entered our hearts in illusion cast  
As a flower appears so briefly in spring,  
We not aware of the separation winter would bring.

Who tells that thou art gone!  
When all around your presence is manifest on  
The myriad pages of instructions divine  
And the crystal clear memories your pastimes define,

When many disciples now qualified preceptors  
Guide new generations beyond illusion’s shores,  
Living Guru seen in the *paramparā,*  
Only your presence is bringing us so far,

When you yourself are perceived  
In the hearts of thousands of devotees who have received  
By disciplic succession from yourself  
Knowledge of Kṛṣṇa, the greatest wealth,

When by your instruction to save many a soul  
Sincere devotees take this order as life’s primary goal,  
Your ISKCON mission spread far and wide,  
It must be you, Śrīla Prabhupāda turning the tide,

When on this day most sacred, the best of all,  
We worship your divine appearance and glories enthrall,  
In return you give us Kṛṣṇa, a precious jewel,  
Your Divine Grace is causeless, there’s no rhyme or rule.

I reason ill—for all these years, Your Grace,  
I still desire to see you one more time, your smiling face  
Just as you were before,  
Our magnificent and fearless leader assaulting Māyā’s score,

Personification of the spiritual energy,  
Drawn into your presence, we no longer felt any apathy,  
Transformed into spirit and burning to please,  
Impossible feats were performed with such ease.

However, you exist in a realm I have not attained.  
The price to reach there is a heart in separation pained.  
Thus to see you again I follow your two lotus feet  
Back to Kṛṣṇa’s abode and your association so sweet.

Your humble servants in Adelaide, Australia.

## Agartala, Tripura, India

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

In spite of many problems of insurgencies and many other difficulties, the devotees here are sincerely working to serve your divine mission. The Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda temple construction plan is progressing nicely by your blessings, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this most auspicious day of your divine appearance, please shower your blessings upon us so that we can humbly serve your divine mission.

Your humble servants,  
The devotees of ISKCON Agartala, Tripura, India.

## Ahmedabad, India

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances at your wonderful lotus feet, which purified the entire earth and planted in even its most sinful inhabitants the seed of the desire tree of the topmost love of Godhead. This extraordinary miracle distinguishes you from all the other followers of Lord Caitanya and Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī.

Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī prayed to Lord Caitanya:

*yo 'jñāna-mattaṁ bhuvanaṁ dayālur*  
*ullāghayann apy akarot pramattam*  
*sva-prema-sampat-sudhayādbhutehaṁ*  
*śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanyam amuṁ prapadye*

**“We offer our respectful obeisances unto that merciful Supreme Personality of Godhead who has converted all three worlds, which were maddened by ignorance, and saved them from their diseased condition by making them mad with the nectar from the treasure-house of love of God. Let us take full shelter of that Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya, whose activities are wonderful. ” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya*****19.54)**

No one in this material world has been more intoxicated in deep ignorance than the twentieth-century cow-killing Western civilization. You extended Lord Caitanya’s mercy to us, the members of this heinous civilization. It is also significant that Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī considered this magnanimous feature of Lord Caitanya (*mahā-vadānya avatāra*) to be the most praiseworthy. This feature of distributing love of Godhead—*kṛṣṇa-prema pradāyate*—is the most intimate feature of the Lord, and He awards the power to similarly distribute this love of God to His most confidential devotee. Here is the description of how Lord Caitanya bestowed upon Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī the blessings of knowing his heart (*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya* 1.66–74):

*daive āsi’ prabhu yabe ūrdhvete cāhilā*  
*cāle goṅjā tāla-patre sei śloka pāilā*

*śloka paḍi’ āche prabhu āviṣṭa ha-iyā*  
*rūpa-gosāñi āsi’ paḍe daṇḍavat hañā*

*uṭhi’ mahāprabhu tāṅre cāpaḍa māriyā*  
*kahite lāgilā kichu kolete kariyā*

*mora ślokera abhiprāya nā jāne kona jane*  
*mora manera kathā tumi jānile kemane?*

*eta bali’ tāṇre bahu prasāda kariyā*  
*svarūpa-gosāñire śloka dekhāila lañā*

*svarūpe puchena prabhu ha-iyā vismite*  
*mora manera kathā rūpa jānila kemate*

*svarūpa kahe,—yāte jānila tomāra mana*  
*tāte jāni,—haya tomāra kṛpāra bhājana*

*prabhu kahe,—tāre āmi santuṣṭa hañā*  
*āliṅgana kailu sarva-śakti sañcāriyā*

*yogya pātra haya gūḍha-rasa-vivecane*  
*tumio kahio tāre gūḍha-rasākhyāne*

**When Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu went to the residence of Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī, He accidentally saw the palm leaf on the roof, and thus He read the verse composed by him. After reading the verse, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu went into an ecstatic mood. While He was in that state, Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī came and immediately fell down on the floor like a rod. When Rūpa Gosvāmī fell down like a rod, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu got up and gave him a slap. Then, taking him on His lap, He began to speak to him. “No one knows the purport of My verse,” Caitanya Mahāprabhu said. “How could you understand My intention?” Saying this, Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu bestowed various benedictions upon Rūpa Gosvāmī, and taking the verse, He later showed it to Svarūpa Gosvāmī. Having shown the verse to Svarūpa Dāmodara with great wonder, Caitanya Mahāprabhu asked him how Rūpa Gosvāmī could understand the intentions of His mind.**

\[PURPORT: We had the opportunity to receive a similar blessing from Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī when we presented an essay at his birthday ceremony. He was so pleased with that essay that he used to call some of his confidential devotees and show it to them. How could we have understood the intentions of Śrīla Prabhupāda?\] Śrīla Svarūpa Dāmodara Gosvāmī replied to Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu, “If Rūpa Gosvāmī can understand Your mind and intentions, he must have Your Lordship’s special benediction.” The Lord said, “I was so pleased with Rūpa Gosvāmī that I embraced him and bestowed upon him all necessary potencies for preaching the bhakti cult. “I accept Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī as quite fit to understand the confidential mellows of devotional service, and I recommend that you explain devotional service to him further.”

We see that you have been similarly blessed by the Lord with his most confidential mission. The Supreme Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu and all His associates have blessed you because you pleased your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Mahārāja. He especially liked your stanza

Absolute is sentient  
Thou has proved,  
Impersonal calamity  
Thou has moved.

You have always attributed your success in spreading Lord Caitanya’s mission to the blessings and satisfaction of your Guru Mahārāja. An astounding fact that has emerged from the history of the Gauḍīya Maṭh is that spiritual success is not so easy. It is very rare. Of thousands of disciples of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, only you, only a single person, really carried forward the mission far and wide in the way he wanted. Even though you have no desire to take credit or criticize anyone, still, as the spiritual master you had to carefully point out this painful fact just so that we may avoid the pitfalls that impeded the progress of Gauḍīya Maṭh members. Lord Kṛṣṇa carefully selects a pure devotee for his mission and empowers him to do extraordinary things. This one thing is evident from your life. Even a blind man can see this.

As the Kaṭha Upaniṣad says:

*nāyam ātmā pravacanena labhyo*  
*na medhayā na bahunā śrutena*  
*yam evaiṣa vṛṇute tena labhyas*  
*tasyaiṣa ātmā vivṛṇute tanuṁ svām*

**“The Supreme Lord is not obtained by expert explanations, by vast intelligence, or even by much hearing. He is obtained only by one whom He Himself chooses. To such a person, He manifests His own form.”**

Now today many Gauḍīya Maṭh *sannyāsīs* are preaching in the West, but they should know they are only eating the remnants of Śrīla Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda. You are very liberal. You don’t mind. You were always willing to invite them and unite all the Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇava preachers, but you were concerned that if they may say one thing different from what you said, they may create havoc. Surely they are saying so many things different today and creating havoc. Of course, they are not the only ones to be blamed. Many of us, your disciples, also have said things different from what you said and similarly created a mess.

You left us a very vibrant living simple and pure spiritual mission. It was so sublime that we really thought we would hop over this *saṁsāra-sāgara* as a matter of sport. But today we have complicated it so much that its very existence is shaken up. We pray to you on this auspicious day to forgive our mistakes. If you may so desire, you may kindly implore your Lord Kṛṣṇa to allow us to continue serving Their Lordships in Their temples, especially in the USA. If you so desire you may also purify our contaminated brains and make us simple and humble instruments in your mission. Once you said to one of your disciples who was somehow thinking he was preaching: “You are not preaching. I am preaching. You are simply assisting me.” Why should we give up your fearless shelter (Abhaya Charan) to increase our false egos and fall down?

The greatest thing that happened in our life, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is that we were fortunate to hear your kind and soothing words. In fact, the greatest thing that happened in the whole last century is that the world has received your immortal books and you have permanently remained in the world by your books and your instructions. The *Bhāgavatam* (11.5.36) says:

*kaliṁ sabhājayanty āryā guṇa-jñāḥ sāra-bhāginaḥ*  
*yatra saṅkīrtanenaiva sarva-svārtho ’bhilabhyate te*

**“Those who are actually advanced in knowledge are able to appreciate the essential value of this Age of Kali. Such enlightened persons worship Kali-yuga because in this fallen age all perfection of life can easily be achieved by the performance of saṅkīrtana.”**

Indeed, if someone is fortunate enough to hear your cent percent transcendental words through your books, his birth in Kali-yuga becomes glorious. Our only prayer on this auspicious day is that we never stop hearing you, life after life. You said there would be an ISKCON in the spiritual world. Thus even after liberation the hearing from the superior devotee never stops. Lord Caitanya told Sanātana Gosvāmī (*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya* 22.83):

*kṛṣṇa-bhakti-janma-mūla haya ‘sādhu-saṅga’*  
*kṛṣṇa-prema janme, teṅho punaḥ mukhya aṅga*

**“The root cause of devotional service to Lord Kṛṣṇa is association with advanced devotees. Even when one’s dormant love for Kṛṣṇa awakens, association with devotees is still most essential.”**

In spiritual life, the association of a pure devotee is all-pervading, from beginning to eternity. That is why Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura says, *ei chay gosāi jār—mui tār dās...janame janame hoy ei abhilāṣ.* It is our desire to remain your servant life after life. Thank you very much for giving us the shade of your lotus feet.

Ever begging for your mercy,  
Your servants at ISKCON Ahmedabad, India.  
(written by Yaśomatīnandana dāsa)

## Amravati, India

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet on this most auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā ceremony, the 105th anniversary of your appearance in this world.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are falling short of words to express our gratitude to you. You are an ocean of limitless mercy, and you have given us so much—more than we deserve.

On this most blessed event of your appearance, we would like to humbly offer you a gift of a new center in Amravati known as Śrī Śrī Rukmiṇī-Dvārakādhīśa Maṇḍir. The Deities are so beautiful that They captivate every heart. They stand four and a half feet high.

We have felt your special mercy upon us as this center has developed and flourished in a very short time. We have our own land, which covers one and a half acres in a very prominent location.

The response for Sunday festivals is tremendous. We have approximately five hundred people at the festival every Sunday. It gives us joy to tell you that many of our congregational members are chanting sixteen rounds every day. We conduct *harināmas* and launch Food for Life programs on a regular basis.

At present the temple team consists of ten to fifteen full-time devotees who are dedicatedly endeavoring to reach out to the famished people with the message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness the way you taught us.

This center has been set up with the encouragement and inspiration of your dear disciple Śrīla Lokanāth Swami Mahārāja.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we beg you on this day to shower us abundantly with your causeless mercy.

Your insignificant servants,  
The devotees of ISKCON Amravati, India.  
(written by Akrūra dāsa)

## Ariel, Israel

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace! All glories to your ISKCON!

On this day we would like to reflect what it means for us to be in ISKCON, why we are in ISKCON, and what we shall do as a part of ISKCON.

First of all—what is ISKCON?

The answer is very simple—ISKCON is your body, ISKCON is your child, and ISKOCN is your life and soul.

If this is the definition of ISKCON, then what does it mean to be in ISKCON?

To be in ISKCON means to serve your body and your soul, it means to be your child at the shelter of your lotus feet. The most love and protection is given to children, and, being your children, we hope to get that love from you through your ISKCON.

Why would we want to be in ISKCON?

The reason to be in ISKCON is to be in your constant association and in the association of those who are your faithful servants—those who are spreading the mission of your life all over the world. To be in ISKCON means to be counted among your soldiers.

And what is the duty of a faithful soldier? The soldier is always ready to give his life for the commander in chief. Śrīla Prabhupāda, your are our commander in chief, and we should be always ready to defend you and your body (ISKCON).

So, on this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we would like to submit the following prayer at your lotus feet:

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please let us always remember these simple truths and let us never forget them.

Your souldiers in Ariel, Israel.  
(written by Yugala-prīti-devī dāsī)

## Baroda, India

*Śrī gurubhyo namaḥ hari oṁ*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your feet. All glories to Śrī Guru and Gaurāṅga!

On the auspicious occasion of Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā, in the twenty-fourth year since your departure from our vision, we pray that you continue to bless the sincere efforts of your servants to uplift the misguided members of human society.

Due to a lack of leadership in human society, humanity in general is ignorant of the real purpose of human life, and instead of following Vedic/Vaiṣṇava-dharma and *varṇāśrama-dharma,* humanity is one the path of sensual enjoyment alone.

Unless humanity, which is ignorant of the real aim of human life, reverses its present misguided course—the course of abusing both the human form of life and the resources of material nature simply for sense gratification—the degradation of humanity will continue and make the world unfit for habitation by those who wish to utilize the rare form of human life to progress toward the eternal abode of Lord Viṣṇu.

You desire to turn the tide of the mad rush of humanity into the abyss. How? By employing multifaceted and multifarious means of propagating the message of the *Bhāgavatam* and other Vedic literatures. This is indeed the panacea—the complete cure—for what ails society.

Because modern education is simply a process for making a man into an animal, Vedic education and knowledge are the alternatives that we must—despite our reluctance to accept them—turn to for our ultimate benefit.

Your books are indeed the basis, or rather the seeds, of this international effort to revive human life on Bhū-maṇḍala.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, may your lofty precepts be our guiding light, and may we not simply present a false show—empty of the important principle that practice is better than precept.

Your servants at ISKCON Baroda, India.  
(written by Bāsu Ghosh dāsa)

## Bhaktivedanta Manor, UK

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Whenever we look around Bhaktivedanta Manor, we see things that remind us of you—the times you spent here with us, and your many instructions on spiritual life. In 1973 when the Manor was first opened, you stayed for many weeks, helping your young disciples. On those warm summer days you liked to talk with them out on the front lawn, sitting on a yellow satin cushion. Years later, when the editors of *Back to Godhead* magazine wanted to publish a formal portrait of you, they offered you a choice of six photographs: you chose the one of yourself sitting on the lawn here at the Manor. We see the lawn today and we think of you. When we drive through the village of Letchmore Heath (or “Lakṣmī-more,” as you called it) we think of you on your morning walks through the village and across the fields. The friendly policeman you spoke with still lives across the road. Your rooms have been kept as they were when you visited. Your dictation machine is there, your bathtub and bed. Even your shaving items are on display. The palanquin on which you were carried in 1977 during your last visit to us, your chair and footstool—all are here. Whenever we see these personal items, we can’t help but think of you.

But we also think of you when we try to do those activities you asked of us. You wanted us to arrange buses to bring guests from all parts of London. Now they come in their hundreds on Sunday and in thousands on festival days. Although we have not arranged coaches, still the coaches come anyway. Every week there are three or four coachloads of children coming from schools all over England. Your Bhaktivedanta Manor is now famous among schoolteachers. You asked that hot *prasādam* always be available for guests and that no one should visit the Manor without receiving some *prasādam*. One time at the Manor in 1975, you personally interrupted your talk to make sure some departing guests received *prasādam*. Now we cook for at least one thousand people on Sundays, and *mahā-prasādam* is always served to each guest who arrives during the week. You said that you wanted a total of ten temples in London. Although we have not reached that figure, there are many groups of practicing and faithful devotees spread across the capital, and the number is growing. You desired that we preach to students so that some of them might come forward to become future ISKCON leaders: now there is preaching in your name going on in seven London universities. You wanted members of the public to come for spiritual instruction: now we are holding classes in many subjects, and hundreds of visitors are learning “techniques of spiritual life.”

Most importantly, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you asked that we should add nothing to your philosophical instructions nor subtract anything. We think that, although we may still have made foolish mistakes, we nevertheless have maintained ourselves as faithful representatives of Your Divine Grace.

Before a building is constructed, the workers must dig the foundation. Although the building eventually grows in an upward direction, the foundation is built downward. This can be confusing for those who know little of building. When you want the building to go up, why dig in the opposite direction? But without a solid foundation, any building will simply collapse at the first tremor. And when the building is intended to be very tall, strong, and long-lasting, then the foundation must be made even deeper. Your careful and detailed instructions on *sādhana,* philosophical teaching, preaching, and management practices constitute the deep foundation for your ISKCON building, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You wanted to build a movement that was strong and powerful and that would last long enough to carry forward the mission you started. You repeated the same instructions on many occasions, on many continents, just so we would understand what you really wanted. At times you may find us to be clumsy servants who struggle to do even simple tasks, but we are confident that you have given us everything we need in this lifetime to awaken our devotional service to Kṛṣṇa. We are a product of your teaching, preaching, guidance, encouragement, and discipline. Everything we know or share with others comes from you, and therefore we owe you everything.

Words cannot adequately express our thanks, but we pray that you will kindly accept our lifetime of service here at your Bhaktivedanta Manor. On your Vyāsa-pūjā day we pledge our continued efforts to share what you have given us so that all of England will one day know your name and realize how great you are.

Your servants at Bhaktivedanta Manor, UK.

## Bogota, Colombia

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances at the dust of your divine lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Here in Colombia we are praying for your mercy. Kali-yuga confuses the people and is most disconcerting. Who knows what will happen next? And I, although your designated “dāsa,” find myself most unfit to tackle such a herculean challenge—that of instilling the sublime mood of true Kṛṣṇa consciousness in steel-framed hearts.

Your books, which are the essence of the literatures meant for swanlike, thoroughly honest men, are indeed meant to create a *viplava,* a veritable revolution in the minds of the denizens of this world’s misdirected “civilization.” One may, however, question: There is already enough violence here—why suggest even more upheaval? But this revolution takes place in the mind! So each of us, blessed by hearing, may vigorously redirect our senses to act rightly. No more not knowing what is to be done, what is not to be done. No more lack of cleanliness, proper behavior, truthfulness. After all, the mind is superior to the senses, the intelligence yet higher, and our very self, the spirit soul, the amazing (*āścaryavat*) part and parcel of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the rightful master of the city of the body. So, rather than violent, this spiritual revolution is uplifting. If there be anger, let us vent it against our rascal lower “self,” soundly beating the mind with a broomstick or big shoe. Thus peace may prevail, as the wild horses of the uncontrolled senses will no longer fight with others’ similarly unbridled faculties. The blind shall no longer lead the blind, the bound shall no longer pretend to free similarly bound fellows. Let the mercy of the Lord, as delivered to us by Your Divine Grace, inundate every nook and cranny to rout out the eternal enemies within.

How to do this? Preach! Please, my divine master, grant me the right and bestow upon me the intelligence to grab the opportunity. This entire planet is in danger! Kindly, compassionately, causelessly, may you bestow on all of your sincere servants here the vision, the determination, the discrimination, and the will to hear and then properly speak *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is*. So long as we are following the process of purification you have given, then “there is no need for hesitation.” We shall chant Hare Kṛṣṇa and fight. Both Māyā and her sponsor, Kali (“Mr. Vice”), shall flee immediately. When will this day be ours?

I personally am a rascal. You know this. I am, in fact, being repeatedly punished for my lack of seriousness. Now perhaps the worst chastisement is having year after year removed from my remaining duration of life. So please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, grant me the great blessing of using every moment in real, useful service.

Please forgive my offenses.

Your servants at ISKCON Bogota, Colombia.  
(written by Prabhupāda dāsa brahmacārī)

## Budapest, Hungary

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

We thank you for not sparing any effort and taking up the almost impossible task of bringing Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the Western world, which was deeply afflicted by materialism. Without your mercy, today there would be nothing valuable in the world outside of India. Even nowadays, the only permanent and useful things in the world are those that are in connection with devotional service to Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Thank your for giving a chance to humanity, blinded by illusion, to understand the final reality, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Thank your for crushing all useless and deluding philosophies in your books with impeccable logic and the statements that strictly follow the Vedic scriptures and Vaiṣṇava *ācāryas*. These philosophies include meaningless materialism, doctrines supporting demigod worship, Māyāvāda doctrines, and concocted, unauthorized religions. Your words penetrate deeply into the hearts of the conditioned souls, and one day they will undoubtedly ripen as devotional service.

Thank your for creating ISKCON, which, like a boat, saves the souls from the ocean of material existence. Thank your for introducing and prescribing the authentic practices of spiritual life, including the regulative principles and the chanting and singing of the Hare Kṛṣṇa mantra. These practices deliver the understanding to every sincere soul that the *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* are not only poetic works but the literature that can save us from the seemingly endless repetition of birth and death.

Thank you for all the service you entrusted to your disciples during your lifetime, and for all the rest of it that has been assigned to us after your disappearance. Without this active engagement, it would be impossible for us to escape Māyā’s attraction.

Thank your for providing those pure and faithful souls who save and spread the bona fide process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in all circumstances. Thank you for our spiritual masters. We especially thank you for the opportunity to associate with His Holiness Śivarāma Swami, who has made Kṛṣṇa consciousness flourish in Hungary, and who deals with the devotees in an unlimitedly personal way.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please help us to always remain faithful to your instructions and to never forget either you or the mission you have entrusted to your followers. We may meet many difficulties in our everyday lives within the movement, and sometimes the attacks of the outside world cause difficulty in spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We pray to you in all humility to give us enough knowledge and intelligence to always understand which of our thoughts concur with your desires and which do not. Please continue helping the fallen souls understand that you are their ever well-wisher, and thus the desire to pursue the path of surrender will awaken in their hearts.

Please continue to grant us shelter at your lotus feet.

The devotees of ISKCON Budapest, Hungary.

## Buenos Aires, Argentina

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

You created a world, you gave us a dream, you sacrificed your health for our happiness, you renounced all material comforts, and you acted as a matchless father. With simple words you presented us with a treasure, you showered upon us majestic blessings and the example of unlimited surrender, you showed unequalled wisdom and told us undecipherable secrets. With the light of the soul and the strength of the heart you gave us, every day, a decisive moment for the change of our lives. You opened the doors of the spiritual sky for us. You united two worlds with transcendental knowledge. You gave us your life and taught us to live. You are our eternal father and master. With unlimited sweetness you filled this swamp with flowers, and with pure greatness you helped us become happy. You are great, you are unlimited, you are unequalled, and you are inseparable from our eternal master, Śrī Kṛṣṇa. In my short life I already know how to act because of what you have taught me. Despite my poor understanding, I know that I will always love you. We love you and you love us. We are sure that Kṛṣṇa is God and that He will always protect us. You did so much that no one could be unhappy. You built an empire, giving love to these poor souls, orphans of God, to these poor bodies that only see material things.

For all this, thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Thank you for taking care of us, understanding us, helping us, and giving us our spiritual life in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Your servants in Buenos Aires, Argentina.  
(written by Kamalā Kṛṣṇa-devī dāsī)

## Cape Town, South Africa

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Recently we asked our devotees here to complete the following sentence: “Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for...” Here’s a sample of the responses we received:

Śikhi Māhiti dāsa: “Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving me such a wonderful opportunity to serve you and your devotees.”

Nanda Kumāra dāsa: “Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving such powerful, moving lectures.”

Mukunda dāsa: “Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for the house in which the whole world can live.”

Prema Kiśora dāsa: “Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for the most valuable gift of devotional service.”

Vrajavāsī dāsa: “Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for making it possible for this fallen rascal to serve you and your devotees.”

Bhakta Yonell: “Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for tolerating me.”

Bhakta Ivor: “Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your divine mercy and for showing me the true objective of life.”

Bhakta Sen: “Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for elevating me to the level of a human being.”

Bhakta Joe: “Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for taking the instructions of your Guru Mahārāja so seriously.”

Bhakta Anesh: “Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for bringing Kṛṣṇa to us.”

Bhakta Matthew: “Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your guidance, wisdom, and love.”

Bhakta Yossi: “Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for caring about me without knowing me.”

Bhakta Ryan: “Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for true love, the love that I have been seeking for so long but could not find.”

Bhakta Julian: “Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for making your devotees more tolerant than a tree.”

Prema-sarovarā-devī dāsī: “Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for allowing me shelter and protection at your lotus feet.”

Maṇikuṇḍala-sevā-devī dāsī: “Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for empowering your representatives, who bring us closer to you.”

Bhaktin Reshmee: “Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your causeless mercy, which does not come easily to the conditioned souls.”

Bhaktin Queeneth: “Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for allowing this fallen soul to be a part of ISKCON”

Bhaktin Arthi: “Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for guiding me through the most confusing time in my life.”

We thought that these statements were in themselves the most perfect glorification of Your Divine Grace. Therefore please accept these sincere and loving appreciations as our Vyāsa-pūjā offering for 2001.

Your humble servants at ISKCON’s Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Māyāpurchandra Maṇḍir in Cape Town, South Africa.

## Chandigarh, India

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. On the occasion of Vyāsa-pūjā we have a wonderful opportunity to glorify your ISKCON and numerous offspring of ISKCON. We are unable to write everything in this one short offering due to the limitation of space. Nevertheless, we can focus on a few this year.

Here in your Chandigarh center, we worship Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava Devjī and Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi with great care and attention. We conduct several festivals for Their pleasure. For Their pleasure we spend our time and energy nicely maintaining Their sweet property, with two big lawns and a garden. We have a small, nice *gośālā*. We have a guesthouse offered to Your Divine Grace by Shri P. L. Sethi. He has also donated a very useful generator to avoid power cuts. He is at present building a facility for running Shree Govinda’s restaurant.

Many youngsters are attracted to the ISKCON Youth Forum. Your Divine Grace’s emphasis on preaching to the youth is proving to be a boon. They are the powerhouse to enlighten the so-called educated youth, who are imitating the materialistic life prominent in the so-called developed countries. Youth Forum members of ISKCON Chandigarh regularly chant, read, preach, distribute your books, clean the temple, help during festivals, conduct classes, participate in house programs, travel to holy places, associate with advanced Vaiṣṇava devotees there, cook and distribute *prasādam,* and keep this center energetic and lively. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we need your causeless mercy to start consistent preaching programs in universities and colleges. We have a large congregation, and about a hundred of them are *harināma* initiated. We are happy to be serving in your ISKCON house at the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava Devjī and Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi.

The devotees of ISKCON Chandigarh, India.

## Christchurch, New Zealand

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

From Vṛndāvana, serenity, you came to a world of shattered dreams, twisted faith, broken hearts. The established patterns insured certain exploitation, but the only alternatives seemed to be continuing to follow the path of passionate entanglement or taking up the path of ignorant destruction. The lack of shelter and trust gave rise to a frustrated mood of fearful violence—demonstrations, bombings, flag-burnings.

But fearlessly you came at the perfect time on the perfect order of a perfect master. To our perfect questions you brought perfect answers and the perfect solution. You entered hell with a simple but perfect program: “Just chant Hare Kṛṣṇa and follow the four regulative principles.” And so you began construction of “the house in which the whole world can live.” Like Govardhanadhārī upholding the huge hill, you produced miraculous shelter.

In this remote place of Christchurch, New Zealand, we continue your struggle, trying to maintain what you have given us and progressing with the new challenges we face. You have bequeathed these challenges so we do not become lazy or take cheaply the sacrifices you made. Although it was easy to follow your faithful leadership, it is now frightening to try to take up a leadership role and direct new devotees whose faith is fragile. You have said, “Just do as I have done.” But as time goes by we are becoming increasingly aware of the reality that no one except you could do what you have done.

Although you have gone on to a greater service, you have left behind the key to the house in which the whole world can live—your books. They keep the door open for all. And although our challenges may now be different, the solution remains the same. The perfect solution to every problem has been meticulously given by you within your books.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please help us hold that door wide open and welcome everyone into your house. Please help us intelligently correct our past and present mistakes. Please help us care for and appreciate all your family members so they will never abandon your house. Please help us continue your perfect example of going out into the world to share Kṛṣṇa consciousness with others. Please help us try to do as you have done.

On this most glorious day of your appearance, we offer our most heartfelt thanks to Śrī Kṛṣṇa for sending you to us.

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!!

Your servants in Christchurch, New Zealand.

## Columbus, Ohio, USA

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

You have performed a historically unparalleled feat by spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world. Your wonderful qualities are the most powerful force behind this. People may not understand fully what you are teaching. But being inspired by your incomparable personality, they are ready to follow you. People are disillusioned by so many role models who turn out to be fallible. After coming into contact with you, they know that there is someone who can lead us, someone who deserves our obedience and veneration. Your extraordinary intelligence, compassion, and discipline make people take you very seriously. With your intelligence you figured out how to turn forty rupees into a worldwide movement of devotees. With your compassion you were willing to clean up after the Bowery youths who attended your programs. With your discipline you never got dragged down to try to enjoy the millions of dollars and thousands of followers that were at your disposal.

Here in Columbus, people from all walks of life are getting inspired by you to advance toward Kṛṣṇa consciousness. They are inspired by reading and hearing your books, lectures, and letters, and also accounts of your activities. These people are professionals, married people, gurukulis, renunciants, students, small children—all sorts of people who, ultimately by your efforts, are becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious. There are a wide variety of devotional services available: *japa, kīrtana,* donating part of a paycheck, cooking, cleaning, distributing books, reading scripture, talking to guests, etc. You have established here an oasis of devotion to Kṛṣṇa where the soul can sing and cherish the shelter of Kṛṣṇa’s friendship. In this fragile, lonely material world, your presence continues to change lives forever. Thank you very much for coming into our lives, and please help us continue to keep you there.

Your servants at ISKCON Columbus, Ohio, USA.

## Coventry, UK

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

We in Coventry are eternally indebted to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your mercy that you continuously bestow upon us, without which we would be helpless. As years go by, the proof of your mercy becomes more evident. Your pioneering skills and your fantastic leadership anchored the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement initially in the West and then all over the world. The formula, the process you left behind to achieve Kṛṣṇa consciousness, works every time. In fact, whenever anyone tries to introduce any new ideas, ideas which they believe are more relevant for the present world climate, they always backfire and we realize that we should stick to the formula you gave.

With the above realization, we in Coventry are re-dedicating our efforts to chant our *japa* in groups, and the outcome has been tremendous. The interpersonal relationships, the group enthusiasm, and the individuals’ spiritual bliss have improved tremendously. Just as you said, “Chant and be happy,” so we are chanting with a more focused mood and we are happier. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

This year Hari dāsa, our temple president, went to the annual GBC meetings and had the opportunity to directly serve Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava and the *aṣṭa-sakhīs*. He was so much inspired by the experience, and he gives all the credit to Your Divine Grace. You gave us the scriptures, the mantra, the spiritual process, the beautiful temples, and the transcendental Deities. We would have been stuck solid in *māyā* if it weren’t for your mercy.

Reading your translated books is spiritually strengthening. By reading them regularly, preaching to others becomes easier. Your purports are so enlightening and clear that doubts have no room in them. You are definitely communicating with us through your books. It is left to us to pick up the books and listen to your guidance. In this way, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we will receive the love of Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa through the mercy of Gaura-Nitāi and yourself. Haribol!

Your humble servants at ISKCON Coventry, UK.Czech Republic

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**I offer my humble obeisances to His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is always fully surrendered at the lotus feet of the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa and is therefore extremely dear to Him.**

**Obeisances to His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, servant of His Divine Grace Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura Prabhupāda. Śrīla Prabhupāda is preaching the gospel of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu, thus delivering the people of the Western world from the doctrines of impersonalism and voidism.**

**I was born into darkness and ignorance, yet out of his mercy the spiritual master opened my eyes with the torchlight of knowledge, and therefore I offer him my humble obeisances.**

I don't want to be a fanatic, a sentimentalist. The spiritual master opened my eyes with the torchlight of knowledge, and it is up to me live with my eyes wide open. If on the contrary I should close them again, after the spiritual master took so much trouble opening them, it would be a pity, a great shame.

Living with eyes open to the spiritual, eternal reality is a matter of consistent devotion to the spiritual master. The very first realization is indebtedness, thankfulness to the spiritual master. If I don’t have this realization, that means that my eyes are completely closed to spiritual reality. *Yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto ’pi*. When I actually realize (*vijñāna*) my debt to the spiritual master, I come to the next step—realizing my relationship with the spiritual master as his servant, his menial servant. Provided my relationship continues even after the physical departure of the spiritual master from my mundane vision, my spiritual vision is intact, my eyes are open. I serve the spiritual master in separation \[*vipralambha-seva*\]. When I become mature in that service, when I love that service as my life and soul, then, and only than, can I come to the realization of what it means to be spiritually alive. I gain positive strength to serve the spiritual master more and more by chanting the holy names and following strictly all the regulative principles. Thus I become a genuine devotee of Lord Kṛṣṇa, because Kṛṣṇa Himself says, “He who says that he’s my devotee is not my devotee. Only he who is a devotee of my devotee is my true devotee.”

Being a devotee of Kṛṣṇa is not a cheap thing, like some fashionable, fanatical sentiment. I’ll become a devotee of Kṛṣṇa only when I realize \[*vijnāna*\] my eternal debt to the spiritual master and become fixed in his service \[*vipralambha-seva*\].

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for opening my eyes with the torchlight of knowledge. I’m slowly realizing, year by year, how I should actually feel indebted to you. At least three times a day I offer my humble obeisances to your lotus feet and pray to always remain your insignificant servant.

The devotees of Kṛṣṇa in the Czech Republic.  
(written by Jaya Gurudeva dāsa)

## Dallas, Texas

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

It is curious why Lord Caitanya chose to wait until the Kali-yuga to give the highest understanding of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. The most esoteric and sublime truths of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, an understanding that had previously been kept secret, are now being given to people who have no qualification or aptitude to receive them. Apparently this doesn't make sense. Mercy has its own sense of rhyme and reason.

The fullest extent of mercy is demonstrated when enormous wealth is given to the poorest, most undeserving people. It was the giving of this mercy to the fallen souls of Kali-yuga that caused Rūpa Gosvāmī to realize that the mercy of Mahāprabhu far surpassed the mercy of any other incarnation of the Lord. And it was Prabodhānanda Sarasvatī who went so far to say that compared to Mahāprabhu, other incarnations of the Lord were virtually inconsequential.

But it is said that Lord Nityānanda was even more merciful because He gave mercy to those who Mahāprabhu would not approach. One of your Godbrothers likened you to Lord Nityānanda. He said that it was certain that you were fully empowered by the Lord to preach, but you were not empowered by just any general potency. Rather, you were specifically empowered by the potency of Lord Nityānanda, because it is that potency which delivers the most fallen souls. You picked up the lowest and put them in the highest position.

If Lord Nityānanda had personally came to the Lower East Side in 1965, the unfolding of history would not have been any different. Since He was actually present within you, whatever He could have done, you did. It appears to some that anyone could have done it. Yes, anyone could have, provided they were fully empowered by Lord Nityānanda. And “anyone” can still do it. But where is that “anyone” to be found? Since your heart was empty of all personal motivations, the Lord’s will fully manifest through you. You often said that you did not feel you did anything wonderful or were in any way a special devotee. But it is only a special devotee, one whose heart is one with the Lord’s mission (*śrī caitanya mano ’bhīṣṭam*), that the Lord fully uses to spread his mission.

It is said that the guru’s mercy is so great that with it a blind man can see stars in the sky, a lame man can cross mountains, and a dumb man can recite eloquent poetry. These phenomena took place in the lives of your disciples as you spread the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement all over the world with their assistance. How you picked up such fallen souls and got them to do so much to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness is truly a miracle. This is testimony to the power of your mercy.

You caused your disciples to aspire for pure devotional service and to work day and night to make the world Kṛṣṇa conscious. These were really not their desires, but they were the desires that you planted deep within their hearts. By the power of your mercy they acted on these desires instead of being let astray by their own.

We know that your mercy, fully endowed with the *śakti* of Nityānanda Prabhu, is still vital today. Thus it can uplift anyone of us, no matter what circumstances we find ourselves in. Any lack or failure in our spiritual lives is due only to our inability to get your mercy.

If we are so unfortunate that we cannot properly avail ourselves of this mercy, despite the fact that you risked your life to give it to each and every one of us, we pray that you will come to us, in some way or another, and force us to take it.

Your servants at Kālachāndjī Dhāma, Dallas, Texas.

## Debe, Trinidad, West Indies

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my respectful obeisances. All glories to you! And all glories to the Brahma-Madhva-Gauḍīya-sampradāya!

In 1962 Your Divine Grace wrote to one Mr. Rickhi (now deceased), a Hindu gentleman who later became a life member. You expressed a desire to come to establish ISKCON in Trinidad. However, Mr. Rickhi broke off corresponding with you and in 1965 you went to New York through Boston and established ISKCON there.

You never physically set foot in Trinidad, but you came in the form of your disciples, your books, your *mūrti,* etc. We feel honored and especially blessed to know that you thought of coming here. We have been doubly blessed because Tulasī-devī also abounds in Trinidad and Tobago.

When some of us joined ISKCON in the mid and late 70’s, we had heard senior devotees quote you as saying that all the people of Trinidad & Tobago would become good devotees of Lord Kṛṣṇa. Even now the devotees come from all kinds of religious and ethnic backgrounds.

In the late 90’s our GBC, His Holiness Guru Prasād Swami, confirmed what we had heard. He told some of us in one meeting that Citsukhānanda Prabhu had personally told him the following:

In the early 70’s, Citsukhānanda had reported to Your Divine Grace that his efforts to spread the message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Trinidad & Tobago were meeting with a good response from the Hindus. Your remark was “Why only the Hindus? The other people will also make good devotees!”

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda! Thank you very much for casting your merciful glance upon us. Without Your Divine Grace we were all doomed.

We pray to Lord Nityānanda Prabhu, whose only desire is to glorify Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu as the most merciful incarnation of Kṛṣṇa Himself, to kindly endow us with the necessary strength and intelligence to fulfill your desire to touch everyone in Trinidad & Tobago (including the aquatics, plants, trees, insects, birds, animals, stones, mountains, and rivers) with Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Since you are the manifestation of Lord Nityānanda’s mercy, we know that this will please you very much.

However, by your divine grace we understand you want us to become steady and determined in our personal practice of Kṛṣṇa consciousness while we attempt to introduce Kṛṣṇa to others.

One motto we have heard numerous times from our authorities is “Books are the basis, preaching is the essence, utility is the principle, and purity is the force.”

Your books are the law books for mankind for the next 10,000 years. Therefore, let us ground ourselves in your transcendental ecstasies, which your books represent.

Your books are identical with your transcendental self. Each page, each line, each word we read or hear is purifying our heart (*guru-mukha-padma-vākya, cittete kariyā aikya*).

When we sit to read your writings, we sit with Your Divine Grace, all the ISKCON devotees, His Divine Grace Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura Prabhupāda, all the predecessor *ācāryas,* Śrīla Śukadeva Gosvāmī, all the *rājarṣis,* all the *mahābhāgavatas,* Arjuna, Lord Kṛṣṇa Himself, Kṛṣṇadāsa Kavirāja Gosvāmī, Lord Caitanya and His eternal associates, etc.

Just as the original unlimited spiritual planet, Goloka Vṛndāvana, manifests Vṛndāvana, India, by Kṛṣṇa’s will, so the Supreme Absolute Truth, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, His abode, and His unlimited energies are manifest in your empowered *vāṇī*!

O how unfortunate we are not to be constantly attuned to Kṛṣṇa, the unlimited Absolute Truth, through your transcendental transparent self!

By your compassionate intercession, may we become worthy of receiving Lord Caitanya’s mercy to become full pure devotees of Kṛṣṇa. And on our way to becoming fully purified, may we become more and more enthusiastic to distribute Kṛṣṇa without discrimination to all other living entities.

By your divine grace, we understand that you want priority to be given to our becoming qualified Vaiṣṇava *brāhmaṇas* and in turn to create greater numbers of such *brāhmaṇas*. By basing our training and education on your books, we are completely assured of becoming genuine Vaiṣṇavas.

Now, even thirty-odd years after the advent of ISKCON in Trinidad & Tobago by you extraordinary mercy, when we meet Muslims, Christians, and others who recognize you and say “Oh, I know this gentleman; I have a book \[or books\] by him,” we feel engladdened. We know, at least to some extent by your grace, how supremely effective is your association through your books. You said, “reading even one line, nay one word of my books can transform one’s life.” *Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda!

May we, by your divine grace, become more and more enthusiastic to preach and distribute your books.

Recently, by your mercy and the co-operative efforts of the devotees, ISKCON Trinidad & Tobago has been incorporated through an act of parliament.

This means ISKCON Trinidad & Tobago qualifies to receive government funding to help establish *gurukulas,* Kṛṣṇa conscious farms (devotees have already purchased 53 acres of land, which are gradually being developed), temples, educational and welfare institutions, etc. Incorporation also gives us the facility to approach and advise the government concerning the welfare of the citizens of Trinidad & Tobago.

We humbly request you to pray to Lord Nityānanda Prabhu to enable us to cooperate nicely to fulfill your pure desire to serve Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura Prabhupāda through the *bṛhat-mṛdaṅga*.

The transcendental writings of the predecessor *ācāryas,* but especially those of Your Divine Grace, are the foundation of the ten-thousand-year golden relief period beginning with Lord Caitanya’s appearance within this dark Kali-yuga.

We are so ignorant. We think we are outnumbered by the nondevotees. The reality is that we are in the majority, ISKCON being part and parcel of the spiritual world (three fourths of the unlimited kingdom of Kṛṣṇa).

O when will that realization become established in our hearts, eradicating all doubt and fear?

Your Divine Grace’s writings are especially significant because of their being circulated in the West (and East, and North, and South). At present, Western materialistic ideals and military strength dominate the East, including India, the only preserver of Vedic, or human, culture.

As Western intelligentsia, by Your Divine Grace’s writings and the efforts of ISKCON devotees, are gradually incorporating the principles of Vedic culture into their lives, more people are being influenced to practice Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Just see how wonderful is Your Divine Grace and how tremendous is Kṛṣṇa! You are no longer physically present, but you are remaining forever in your books. We are reminded of Lord Jesus Christ’s declaration: “Heaven and earth will pass away but not one jot of my words will pass away.”

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda! All glories to you again and again! All obeisances to you again and again!

Thank you very much for so kindly allowing us miscreants to practice Kṛṣṇa consciousness and to assist Your Divine Grace in your preaching mission. May we not disappoint Your Divine Grace.

*Jagat-guru* Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

Your followers in Debe, Trinidad, West Indies.  
(written by the ISKCON Trinidad & Tobago National Board)

## Debrecen, Hungary

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Please accept our respectful obeisances! All glories to Your Divine Grace! And all glories to Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi and to all *saṅkīrtana* devotees!

On this wonderful day—Your most merciful appearance day, full of deep meaning—please allow us to glorify you with a few words. Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is a very difficult task to describe your endless glories in a few sentences in limited time. Many people think that you are an eminent scholar of spiritualism, or an honorable, followable person in this contaminated, confused world. But the devotees who are serious to follow your instructions all know without any speculation that you are much more than that. You took responsibility for establishing the worldwide *saṅkīrtana* movement that will become predominant for the next ten thousand years. Your explanations and instructions are codes of conduct for intelligent human beings. Your directions are the guidance of the Supreme Person, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, Himself. You never acted for any self-interest. You always labored to give the best to others. Your goal was to rescue others from the depths of ignorance, and your desire was one with that of Kṛṣṇa, and as an intimate associate of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu you saved the world.

Now it is our chance to act. We have to demonstrate to Kṛṣṇa that we want to be your pure-hearted servants. We often hear that Kṛṣṇa accepts only those souls who want to be the servants of the servants of the servants of the spiritual masters.

We have to cooperate, and we have to be the servant of all devotees, not only the most advanced devotees, nor should we think we can submit ourselves only to Kṛṣṇa and not to you or your representatives. Please help us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to grow in this way and satisfy you by your limitless mercy.

Your eternal servants at the ISKCON Temple in Debrecen, Hungary.

## Denmark

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet. Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

I am writing this offering to you today on the appearance day of His Divine Grace Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura. We have been reading the lectures you gave on this auspicious occasion. How fortunate we are to have you as our guide and preceptor! You have taken the instructions of your Guru Mahārāja and fulfilled his heart’s desire. If by the example you have shown us of following the order of the spiritual master we too can attain the mercy of your lotus feet, our endeavors will be successful. I humbly pray that you will ever be merciful to us here in Denmark so that whatever we do will be based on your instructions. Our hope is to continually glorify you and in so doing perfect our lives.

This year will see us moving the temple from the northern suburbs of Copenhagen into Copenhagen proper. All our endeavors are simply to please you; we hope this move will lead to an explosion of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Denmark. Please bless us so that we will attain success in this mission. By the mercy of guru one can get Kṛṣṇa, but without that mercy all our attempts are futile.

You said that Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī is *gaura-śakti*. *Gaura-śakti,* the potency of Caitanya Mahāprabhu. We can see that you are also *gaura-śakti,* for you have presented so many wonderful books for the eternal benefit of the conditioned souls, who without your mercy would remain in the lap of Māyā. Our hope is to please you with the spreading of the holy names and your books, which are beacons of light to all those who are sincere to find their way home. Saying “Thank you” is not enough for the gift you have given us all.

Your servants at ISKCON Denmark.  
(written by Ātmātma dāsa)

## Denver

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you, my divine master, on this most auspicious day of your appearance!

Another year has past, and my anxiety has only increased as I wonder when I will become sincere about serving your lotus feet. I am feeling myself very inadequate in expressing my disappointment at not having been able to seriously take up your request to serve the mission of Lord Caitanya in spreading the *saṅkīrtana* movement. Instead I am becoming more engrossed in the bodily concept of life, simply thinking about home, family, wealth, etc. I know that these can be used in Kṛṣṇa’s service, but I have abused them, thinking they are meant for my gratification. Under the pretense that I need these for Kṛṣṇa’s service, I have become complacent, forgetting that this life is so short and meant for sacrifice, as you showed by your personal example. Incredibly, some persons have even criticized that example of *vairāgya-vidyā,* equating it with some mundane dissatisfaction or sentimentality. Just as Lord Caitanya was criticized for His renunciation and His devotion but later revealed his true glory and allowed His devotees to enter into His transcendental pastimes, some have questioned your motives and spiritual understandings, but what is their qualification?

*Dṛṣṭaiḥ svabhāva-janitair vapuṣaś ca doṣair*. One should be careful not to see you from a materialistic point of view. There is no doubt in my mind that you are a Vaikuṇṭha man and that out of great compassion you descended from the abode of Lord Govinda to bestow His mercy upon us, teaching us the highest and most intimate truth through your books. If someone cannot see this, it is only out of dull-headedness or envy, which I’m sorry to say I am a victim of. We have heard and seen so many proofs of your divine nature, and yet some can see you only as some historical figure because they haven’t tasted the nectar of your service. How can one who is under the grips of the modes of nature understand a transcendental personality?

*Vaiṣṇavera kriyā, mudrā vijñeha nā bujhaya*. It is not by birthright, wealth, knowledge, or beauty that one becomes qualified to receive the mercy of God or understand the activities of liberated souls. Instead, it is by accepting responsibility for our own suffering and humbly approaching a liberated soul and begging for his mercy. I accept the fact that I have not been a very good disciple and have squandered the rare opportunity to serve your lotus feet, but one thing is for sure: I will never leave those lotus feet. So I am begging you to please exhibit your glory again by helping this suffering soul. This will once again prove your exalted position and the mercy of Lord Caitanya. I remember the few times that I had your association and how, because I was so covered over, I did not take advantage of them. Please give me your mercy so that I may never forget those times.

*Tvaṁ naḥ sandarśito dhātrā dustaraṁ nistitīrṣatām*. *(Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.1.22) Certainly it was by the will of providence that we were able to meet Your Divine Grace and be given the opportunity to assist you. Having completely forgotten the aim of life, and out of madness engaging in horrible, sinful activities in this brief life, we would still be drowning had it not been for your rain cloud of causeless mercy. In *Bhagavad-gītā* 5.18Lord Kṛṣṇa describes you perfectly: *paṇḍitāḥ sama-darśinaḥ*. You are that humble sage who saw beyond our material conditioning, encouraging us and all the while tolerating the trouble we created for you in our feeble attempts to serve you. Lord Kapila states (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 3.25.23), *mad-āśrayāḥ kathā mṛṣṭāḥ śṛṇvanti kathayanti ca*/ *tapanti vividhās tāpā naitān mad-gata-cetasaḥ*. “Engaged constantly in chanting and hearing about Me, the *sādhus* do not suffer from material miseries because they are always filled with thoughts of My pastimes and activities.” Still, we are very sorry for our incompetence and lack of sincerity. Because your heart is completely filled with love for the Personality of Godhead, you are purifying even the most hellish places. Our hope is that we can make a suitable place in our hearts for your lotus feet, so that we can become relieved of this burning material existence. Fools that we are in trying to avoid this burning of material desires, we have jumped into the dangerous ocean of the Age of Kali, which is full of the sharks of *jñāna* and yoga. Somehow, by the Lord’s infinite mercy you have come and plucked us out of this nightmare condition and placed us upon the boat of transcendental knowledge, and you are guiding it perfectly. Just as the holy name can penetrate through all the material coverings, there are no boundaries to the effects of your preaching, in which you are giving the topmost truths to the most unqualified. We certainly accept you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, as our captain, and our fervent prayer is that you will accept me as one of your crew.

Your eternal servants at ISKCON Denver, USA.  
(written by Naikātmā dāsa)

## Detroit

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Our most beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Today all the devotees at the Devasadan Mandir in Detroit are celebrating the 105thanniversary of your appearance day with the greatest happiness and joy, especially since it also coincides with the 25thanniversary of your last visit here and your installation of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi. We feel you are always with us, as you left so many memories of your pastimes and instructions during your visits here.

During the past year, so many things have happened. Some of them very good, some not so good. But because of your blessings and protection, we are able to continue your mission without hindrance. Your temple is nicely managed by your disciples and disciples of your disciples, with great cooperation and support for one another in their service. During the tough time, we appreciated every single devotee who is helping and participating in your mission. All the transcendental festivals you gave us always bring auspiciousness and joyous times for all of us at the temple, as well as the guests. We had a very successful Janmāṣṭamī, Ratha-yātrā, Govardhana Pūjā, Tulasī Vivāha, Gaura Pūrṇimā, and twelve-hour Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra kīrtana* programs at the temple.

The month of December is especially very blissful for all the devotees, as it starts with your book distribution marathon and ends on the 31stof December with the twelve-hour *kīrtana*. Only by your mercy are devotees fired up to distribute books to any soul they see. We have started several new ways to increase our book distribution, and we need your blessing to be successful in that.

One way is that we request congregational members and guests to fill out request cards for your books at the public libraries where they live. This program is enlarging slowly, and it has a multiple effect. Our members become your representatives to their local libraries and thus feel part of your movement. Sometimes they are so much inspired that they even buy your books and give them to the libraries as a gift or donation. Thus our book distribution also increases. Gradually, we would like to fill all the libraries with your books in this way. We have high hopes for this, since in the USA libraries honor the requests of their taxpayer citizens quickly. We also find that because many of our previous leaders have done a wonderful job, many libraries already have your books.

We got the idea for our second new way to increase book distribution when we learned that in North America more than 70% of the motels are owned by members of our Indian heritage. So we are requesting each of them to buy your *Śrīmad Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* and place it in every room along with the Bible. In this way, the motel owners get benefit, and those who will have the good fortune to read your *Bhagavad-gītā* will also receive tremendous mercy. We have found that you have already planted the seeds in the hearts of the motel owners, the majority of whom happen to be from Surat, the city you blessed with your Shoba Yātrā and lectures in 1970. So now our job is to approach them nicely and give them your mercy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are most fallen and bewildered in this material world. We do not know how we deserve to receive such a great opportunity to participate in your mission. We have only one prayer to you: Please keep us always engaged in the service of your lotus feet. We are very thankful to you for giving us books, festivals, temples all around the world, and, best of all, so many of your wonderful disciples and grand-disciples to give us Vaiṣṇava association anywhere we go.

Please forgive us for all the offenses we have committed knowingly or unknowingly, either to you or to some of your devotees.

Your unworthy servants at the Devasadan Mandir, Detroit, Michigan, USA.  
(written by Jagad Guru dāsa)

## East Hartford, Connecticut, USA.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

It is time to write a Vyāsa-pūjā offering, and since we are absorbed in the relative world, we are thinking of how you can be compared with others here. But we can find no one to compare you with among any of the people we have ever come in contact with. You are in a class of your own. You are “a Vaikuṇṭha man,” as you described your Guru Mahārāja, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī. It is evident that you are not of this relative, mundane world.

Saints of your caliber are themselves places of pilgrimage. Because of their purity, they are constant companions of the Lord, and therefore they can purify even the places of pilgrimage. \[*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.13.10, quoted from *Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi*1.63\]

We can compare you with Śrīla Vyāsadeva, the person for whom the Vyāsa-pūjā offerings were first started. He spoke the Vedic literatures for the benefit of the fallen souls in this Age of Kali, and you translated them for the benefit of those same fallen souls. We can compare you with Nārada Muni, who travels throughout the universe preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness, because you traveled the world several times for the sole purpose of preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness. He would glorify the Lord while playing his *vīṇā,* as you did with *karatālas, mṛdaṅga,* and harmonium. We can also compare you with Bhīṣmadeva, who preached the absolute truth even while lying on a bed of arrows. You never stopped preaching, even while lying on your disappearance bed in Vṛndāvana. We can only compare you with great *mahājanas* and liberated incarnations. There is no comparing you with anyone or anything of this age. You are not a product of Kali-yuga. In you, Kali’s influence is conspicuous by his absence.

O learned one, in this iron age of Kali men have but short lives. They are quarrelsome, lazy, misguided, unlucky and, above all, always disturbed. \[*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*1.1.10\]

You are more like someone from a different age who entered into this Kali-yuga to help the fallen souls go back home, back to Godhead.

“Those who are devoted to the cause of the Personality of Godhead live only for the welfare, development, and happiness of others. They do not live for any selfish interest....” (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.4.12)

We are forever indebted to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and we are certain that your constant companion, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, is most pleased with you. Wherever you came from is not very important to us, but what is important is that you did come and guide us in the right direction.

We think that we have met Your Goodness by the will of providence, just so that we may accept you as captain of the ship for those who desire to cross the difficult ocean of Kali, which deteriorates all the good qualities of a human being. \[*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.1.22\]

Having had “a moment” of your association is enough to convince us that wherever you are, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is where we want to be. We hope to someday, somehow qualify ourselves to associate with you and your associates.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for all the sacrifices you made for us. We hope we can please you by making sacrifices for you every day, for the rest of our lives.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Hoping against hope for your mercy, we remain  
Your servants in East Hartford, Connecticut, USA.  
(written by Pyāri Mohan dāsa and Jīvanauṣadhī-devī dāsī)

## Eger, Hungary

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Please accept my respectful obeisances. All glories to you!

We’d like to introduce ourselves briefly. We’re living and preaching in a small Hungarian town called Eger. We have a center near downtown. We have twenty-five full-time devotees and 150 to 200 congregational members around the city. We also have a little restaurant in the center, where many people come to take *prasādam*. We have a Food for Life program, through which we distribute *prasādam* to 100 people every week. We also have a college program weekly: philosophy and *prasādam*. We have a Sunday program also, in which all the full-time Vaiṣṇavas and the congregational members participate in the celebration. And most important: we distribute your books in the town and the nearby counties.

These devotional services are all manifestations of your divine grace. You came to the Western countries to save all the *mlecchas* and *yavanas* by engaging them in devotional service to the Lord. How can we glorify you better than by showing the results of your preaching? The results are we ourselves, who were *mlecchas* and *yavanas* but who by your mercy can now act like Vaiṣṇavas.

You are so great that you can save everyone and anyone by your preaching throughout the world. It is unparalleled, and we can understand that your mercy and potency are unlimited. You have Kṛṣṇa, and you want to give Him to everyone.

You have empowered your disciples and the disciples of your disciples to go door to door to enlighten the people of Kali-yuga. By their personal example they teach people how to act properly. They don’t take anything for themselves but just try to save others. They do it on your order: preach and save others. They’re so determined because they have such a great spiritual master as you who are tireless in your service: to take everyone back home to Godhead.

We’re very thankful to you for saving us from material life and giving us this wonderful process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We promise that we will try to do our best to be Kṛṣṇa conscious and spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We especially like to read your books, knowing that this was one of your only complaints—that your devotees did not read your books enough.

Please help us to practice and preach as you want us to!

Thank you very much for your kind attention and for the work you did for us.

Your servants in Eger, Hungary.  
(written by Caitanya dāsa)

## El Salvador

Worshipable Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Your unfathomable compassion is saving all the living entities who have the good fortune to come in contact with your International Society for Krishna Consciousness, whether they be human beings, animals, or plants.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, in your purports you have explained that it is very difficult to comprehend the activities of a pure devotee. Since you established ISKCON in the Western world, through the years we have realized that we have had insufficient understanding of your pure desire to fulfill the mission of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu. The huge task of your ISKCON is overburdening our tiny efforts, although your sincere disciples and the sincere disciples of your disciples are doing the best they can to accomplish this goal.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, in your purports you say that Kali-yuga is an ocean of faults and all the demoniac qualities are very prominent. In the Sixteenth Chapter of the *Bhagavad-gītā,* Kṛṣṇa conclusively explains these qualities to Arjuna:

Pride, arrogance, conceit, anger, harshness and ignorance—these qualities belong to those of demoniac nature, O son of Pṛthā....Those who are demoniac do not know what is to be done and what is not to be done. Neither cleanliness nor proper behavior nor truth is found in them....Taking shelter of insatiable lust and absorbed in the conceit of pride and false prestige, the demoniac, thus illusioned, are always sworn to unclean work, attracted by the impermanent....Perplexed by various anxieties and bound by a network of illusions, they become too strongly attached to sense enjoyment and fall down into hell....Self-complacent and always impudent, deluded by wealth and false prestige, they sometimes proudly perform sacrifices in name only, without following any rules or regulations.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, whenever we think of the potency you emanate through your ISKCON, we become confident that anything arrayed against it, such as these demoniac qualities, cannot affect your International Society for Krishna Consciousness; rather they will fade away by your glories.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are trying to learn how to love you; nonetheless we become overwhelmed by your pure love.

Your insignificant servants at ISKCON El Salvador.

## Ghana

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Let us offer our respectful and most humble obeisances unto the lotus feet of His Divine Grace Śrīla A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having taken full shelter at His lotus feet. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you kindly descended into this material world just to dispel with the teachings of Lord Caitanya the darkness of ignorance that has engulfed the entire planet in the form of gross materialism, impersonalism, and voidism. You have so expertly done this by your fearless and uncompromising preaching, and this has endeared you to Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura.

Today marks yet another anniversary of your divine appearance in this universe. Exalted personalities chant wonderful prayers to herald your divine appearance as well as glorify your uncommon pastimes.
