This is certainly an auspicious moment for the entire world, and it is especially ecstatic for the spiritually-minded, for your appearance marked the beginning of a spiritual revolution to usher in the Satya-yuga in Kali-yuga. The people of this world owe you volumes of gratitude for providing us with spiritual ammunition in the form of your transcendental literatures, with which we can wage the all-important spiritual warfare against the forces of darkness. Although you have left behind great spiritual assets for your spiritual family of disciples and grand-disciples, the family is going through serious upheavals due to the infiltration into the camp of sinister persons who lack the needed detachment, sincerity of purpose, and readiness to sacrifice for the benefit of others.

On this most auspicious occasion of your divine appearance, we fervently pray that Your Divine Grace will kindly empower, guide, and protect your sincere followers so they can counter all the vile attacks and ridicule from such detractors. Simultaneously, we also pray that Your Divine Grace free such persons from their dangerous motives so that together we can spread Lord Caitanya’s mission to every town and village.

Your unqualified grand-disciples in Ghana.  
(written by Śrīvāsa dāsa)

## Gītā Nāgarī Baru farm in Lampung, Indonesia

Hare Kṛṣṇa!

All glories to Śrī Śrī Guru and Gaurāṅga!  
All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances at your lotus feet. Śrīla Prabhupāda, because of your mercy, all of us are performing devotional service to Kṛṣṇa. Since 1999 we have been trying to follow your instructions for developing this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. Now we have the a large piece of land (sixty hectares) at Tulang Bawang, Lampung, Indonesia. This place has been given the name Gītā Nāgarī Baru.

Eight families are now staying at Gītā Nāgarī Baru, and very soon the Rādhā–Madana-Gopāla temple will be completely built. In all things, we are trying to follow the Vedic culture. Gītā Nāgarī Baru village is without electricity. Some food crops are coming from our own land. We are trying to depend on the land and the cows.

All of this is actually your desire, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We do not depend on modern things. We will develop the traditional ways according to the Vedic culture.

This project has been directed by His Holiness Bhakti Rāghava Swami. We try to follow him in order to please you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and to please Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

In the beginning, there were some difficulties. But slowly, by the mercy of Guru and Kṛṣṇa, we must fix everything for your satisfaction, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Actually, in all people’s lifestyles, especially devotees, they should be thinking how they can go back to the Vedic lifestyle. Now in most places people are depending on industry and modern things. But in Gītā Nāgarī Baru, we are changing. We will live naturally in everything.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants at the Gītā Nāgarī Baru farm in Lampung, Indonesia.  
(written by Ṣaḍbhuja Gaurāṅga dāsa)

## Gītā Nāgarī Farm, Port Royal, Pennsylvania, USA

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

On this auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we are deeply grateful to you for your example of absolute dedication to your spiritual master, Śrī Śrīmad Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, a dedication you showed by fulfilling his order to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the Western world.

In *Mārkine Bhāgavata-dharma* (your prayers for deliverance of all fallen souls), you write:

*akhila jagat guru! bacana se āmār*  
*alaṅkṛta koribār khamatā tomār*

**O spiritual master of all the world,**  
**I can simply repeat Your message,**  
**so if You like You can make my power**  
**of speaking suitable for their understanding.**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by the power of your speaking, fools and rascals are taking shelter and caring for Their most beautiful Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Dāmodara, Rādhā-Kālachandjī, Śrī Śrī Jagannātha, Baladeva, Subhadrā, Śrīmatī Viśākhā-devī, Śrīmatī Lalitā-devī, and Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurasundara.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by the power of your speaking, your sons His Holiness Bhakti Tīrtha Swami and His Holiness. Satsvarūpa Mahārāja inspired the assembled devotees to complete a gorgeous new temple for Your Divine Grace and Their Lordships—a project that had been on hold for over six years.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by the power of your speaking, cow protection at Gītā Nāgarī is being fulfilled through surrendered devotees who are lovingly caring for your cows;

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by the power of your speaking, the many children present here are being trained in the culture of devotion;

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by the power of your speaking, we have a weekly college preaching program where your transcendental books and *prasādam* are being distributed;

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by the power of your speaking, a book trust is now being set up to place your transcendental books in libraries across the country;

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by the power of your speaking, we are coordinating many festivals, retreats, and workshops throughout the year, where the vibration of the holy names can sound very loudly and clearly.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, recently at a gathering to view your latest Prabhupāda memory tape (#28 in the series), one of your sons shared that you spoke of coming to Gītā Nāgarī to sit and teach devotees how to live off the land. The desires of pure devotees are always fulfilled, and thus we are fully aware that you are here with us in your *arcā-vigraha* form, and even though we are utterly disqualified by our citified consciousness, we know that the power of your speaking has been suitable for our understanding. Thus, we are begging to learn to listen with increasing attention, Śrīla Prabhupāda, so that you can continue to teach us everything you want to impart, including how to live off the land.

Your children and grandchildren at the Gītā Nāgarī Farm Community in Port Royal, Pennsylvania, USA.

## Gṭthenburg, Sweden

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances!

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us the association of your sincere followers!

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us the holy name, the most valuable gift of all gifts!

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us your books to read, to study, and to distribute. It is very sad for us and the world that you are not present here in your *vapu* form to guide us anymore. But through your books we can still have your direct association, and we can also give your association to other fortunate souls. That is our good fortune!

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us this wonderful preaching mission. By taking shelter of your mission, we have unlimited opportunities to perform devotional service for the rest of our lives. That is our good fortune!

Please let us always stay in the association of your sincere followers, and please help us become the humble servants of all your servants.

Your humble servants at the Gṭthenburg preaching center in Sweden.

## Hong Kong

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace and to the whole transcendental *paramparā*! All glories to ISKCON, your vehicle for saving the conditioned souls of Kali-yuga!

In the *Bhagavad-gītā* (4.40) it is stated: “But ignorant and faithless persons who doubt the revealed sciptures do not attain God consciousness; they fall down. For the doubting soul there is happiness neither in this world nor the next.”

For Kali-yuga residents like us, it is quite natural to have the inauspicious qualities mentioned above, namely ignorance, faithlessness, and doubt. If unchecked, these demonlike qualities kill the spiritualist’s progress.

Your Divine Grace, out of great mercy, appeared in this world to give knowledge and the process for freeing us from these obstacles so that we may approach the goal of our lives. We are eternally indebted to Your Divine Grace and your sincere followers. We need your mercy to stay on the devotional path.

Your further explain in the *Bhagavad-gītā* (16.1–3): “Fearlessness, purification of one’s existence, cultivation of spiritual knowledge....These transcendental qualities, O son of Bharata, belong to godly men endowed with divine nature.”

We are so much in ignorance that it takes us quite some years to realize that we were not born with these transcendental qualities. In fact, we were born with just the opposite qualities, that is, pride, arrogance, conceit, anger, etc.So, practically speaking it is a struggle every day to practice spiritual life, either individually or collectively. You are our fearless founder-*ācārya,* the *sākṣād dharitvena*—direct representative of Kṛṣṇa—who appeared in order to give us the mercy to come closer to the Lord despite our disqualifications. We sincerely pray to your divine lotus feet that you may bestow your mercy upon us so that we can purify our morbid existence and become steady in the process of acquiring transcendental knowledge, etc. In other words, we pray that you empower us to take full shelter of this transcendental process, and hopefully one day our gratitude will be aroused to such a degree that we will appreciate your unlimited kindness and will attempt to do something to help the suffering human society, as our service to Your Divine Grace.

Praying again to follow your footsteps through the mercy of your sincere followers, we remain

Your servants in Hong Kong.  
(written by Nārāyaṇa dāsa)

## Indore, India

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble and prostrated obeisances.

The following prayer was originally written for your Guru Mahārāja, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, but we find it equally appropriate for you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. So we begin this humble offering with this selected prayer for your transcendental pleasure:

*sujanārbuda rādhita pāda-yugaṁ*  
*yuga-dharma durandhara patra-varam*  
*varadābhaya-dāyaka pūjya-padam*  
*pranamāmi sadā prabhupāda padam*

**“O Śrīla Prabhupāda, your beautiful lotus feet are cherished by millions and millions of the purest and most qualified devotees, and you are the most competent personality to preach the recognized process of dharma for this era. Your sacred lotus feet are adorable, as they openly grant fearlessness and bestow the highest benediction upon all living entities. I eternally offer my respects unto the charming effulgence that shines forth from the lotus toe-tips of Śrīla Prabhupāda.”**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you never accepted any credit for your unprecedented achievements but gave full credit to Kṛṣṇa and your beloved Guru Mahārāja, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura: “Kṛṣṇa has been so kind upon me to have sent so many sincere disciples to help me push on this Movement on behalf of my Guru Mahārāja.” And because of your total surrender to your Guru Mahārāja’s lotus feet, you have fulfilled the mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have created all good fortune for all of humanity and revealed, amongst many other gems, the transcendental and invaluable gem of Śrī Vṛndāvana-dhāma.

*ārādhyo bhagavān vrajeśa-tanayas tad-dhāma vṛndāvanaṁ*  
*ramya kācid upāsana vraja-vadhū-vargeṇā va kalpitā*

**“As the Lord is worshipable, so is His dhāma, and among all modes of devotional service, the mode of worship of the gopīs is the highest.”**

Being wicked, though we received the precious jewel of *bhakti,* we kept it in the hem of our cloths. Unaware of the value of your instructions and association, we were searching out sea-shells, tiny specks of false happiness on the shore of mundane activities. Not diving into the ocean of *bhakti,* we have been lethargic. We relish bitter-tasting, deceitful gossip as nectar but remain apathetic to hearing the name, qualities, and pastimes of the Lord. We are so fallen! But you always and repeatedly bestow undeserved favors upon us.

Therefore, as Prahlāda Mahārāja says:

*evaṁ janaṁ nipatitaṁ prabhavāhi-kūpe*  
*kāmābhikāmam anu yaḥ prapatan prasaṅgāt*  
*kṛtvātmasāt surarṣiṇā bhagavan gṛhītaḥ*  
*so ’haṁ kathaṁ nu visṛje tava bhṛtya-sevām*

**“My dear Lord, O Supreme Personality of Godhead, because of my association with material desires, one after another, I was gradually falling into a blind well full of snakes, following the general populace. But Your servant Nārada Muni kindly accepted me as his disciple and instructed me how to achieve this transcendental position. Therefore, my first duty is to serve him. How could I leave his service?”**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this divine day of your appearance, we resolve to always remain attached to your lotus feet and not leave you in any circumstance.

We also resolve to strive to make ISKCON an impenetrable fort so that Māyā and her agents do not cause disturbances.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, Lord Kṛṣṇa is all sweet and the very source of sweetness:

*adharaṁ madhuraṁ vadanaṁ madhuraṁ*  
*nayanaṁ madhuraṁ hasitaṁ madhuraṁ*  
*hṛdayaṁ madhuraṁ gamanaṁ madhuraṁ*  
*madhurādhi-pater akhilaṁ madhuraṁ*

**“His lips are sweet, His face is sweet His eyes are sweet, His smile is sweet His heart is sweet, His gait is sweet—everything is sweet about the original Lord of Sweetness.”**

In the same way, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are you an abode of all sweet qualities.

Yet it is a wonder that, ignorant and worthless as we are, can’t appreciate this.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, the debt we owe you for the invaluable jewel of *bhakti* you have given us cannot be repaid in a million lives. Yet we can please you by taking to preaching seriously. Even while in a situation of discomfort and illness in Vṛndāvana, you continued like a soldier does. You said that a soldier never gives up fighting. Even if his head is severed, he continues to wave his sword so that he may kill or injure his enemy.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this divine day of your appearance we offer you a beautiful garland. This garland is made of the flowers of your flowerlike devotees, as you compared even insignificant persons in devotional service to flowers. This garland of flowers is strung by a wonderful and transcendental string of your *amṛta-vāṇī,* your divine and nectarean instructions. This string of your *vāṇī* keeps us always united, and this garland remains ever fresh.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you know the hearts of all your devotees, and your presence is always soothing for all. Once Tejiyas das was feeling dejected because he was not able to do big projects. You encouraged him by saying that it is not that we simply take up big, big projects and in due course feel discouraged. We must engage in some service because that is the practical application of our Kṛṣṇa consciousness. But we can be successful even if we preach to just one person per day. This is a valuable instruction to us: it is not big projects that matter but our mood in executing Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, finally, *śūnyāyitaṁ jagat sarvaṁ tvaṁ-viraheṇa me.*  
Your servants in Indore, India.

## Kecskemet, Hungary

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances! All glories to Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s *saṅkīrtana* mission!

A couple of years ago, on the occasion of your appearance day, my spiritual master said, “Kṛṣṇa consciousness means Prabhupāda consciousness.” Therefore we, your followers, should remember and glorify you, since Your Divine Grace enabled us fallen souls in the Western world to become acquainted with the real science of God.

This auspicious day is very important for all of your followers and disciples worldwide, who render you special worship on this day. They all appreciate the great effort you undertook to found the many centers of Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the desert of this world. As you wrote in a letter:

The more we open branches, the more I feel encouraged. These branches are like the oasis in a vast desert. In the desert there is no water, but occasionally if one is fortunate he may come in contact with an oasis and he is saved. Similarly in this material world we are drying up due to lack of spiritual knowledge. Our ISKCON centers are meant to give relief to the dried up conditioned souls who are searching after the nectar of joyful life....\[Letter to Bhagavān dāsa, 18 December 1970\]

With your books and devoted disciples you have raised us up and empowered us to become preachers of your message. Though our abilities are limited, we experience that people appreciate your teachings very much. We are a small center at present, but we hope we’ll attract more and more people to engage in the service of your lotus feet. Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is wonderful to see how your spiritual strength acts, even without your physical presence, to change people’s lives. We regularly engage in your mission of book distribution, and in this way, by rendering service, we find that your teachings become practice and we become more determined in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for allowing us to be part of your wonderful mission. In your letters you write that every center is your life and soul. We pray to you that we can satisfy you by our endeavors and that we will always be absorbed in your loving service.

Your insignificant servants at the ISKCON center in Kecskemet, Hungary.  
(written by Dāmodara dasa)

## Kiev, Ukraine

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your holy feet. All glories to Śrī Guru and Gaurāṅga!

*yat-pāda-saṁśrayāḥ sūta munayaḥ praśamāyanāḥ*  
*sadyaḥ punanty upaspṛṣṭāḥ svardhuny-āpo ’nusevayā*

**“O Sūta, those great sages who have completely taken shelter of the lotus feet of the Lord can at once sanctify those who come in touch with them, whereas the waters of the Ganges can sanctify only after prolonged use.”**

In the purport to this famous verse from *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, you write:

The pure devotees whose only business is serving, are honored by the names Prabhupāda and Viṣṇupāda, which indicates such devotees to be representatives of the Lord....Such devotees of the lord are honored equally with the Lord because they are engaged in the most confidential service of the Lord, for they deliver out of the material world the fallen souls whom the Lord wants to return home, back to Godhead.

A person who is spiritually receptive will see on the basis of your life’s activity the demonstration of how you were dedicated to this most confidential service of giving deliverance and vision to those who are *paśyann api na paśyati,* seeing but not seeing. You, being a *kṛpā-śakti-mūrti,* personified form of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s mercy, bestowed upon us divine eyes, which are considered much more valuable than the external sight given by the sun. The sun can give us sunlight only when it is risen in the sky, but through your books, lectures, *bhajans,* and servants you are perpetually giving us the rays of *kṛpā,* which expand from your humble heart saturated with compassion and dedication to the mission of your spiritual master and Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Before coming in contact with your Divine Grace we were on the bottom of the ocean of ignorance, which is full of suffering and misfortune. Our fortune had been devoured by various modern speculative philosophies. *Yās tu śrutā hata-bhagair nṛbhir ātta-sārāḥ:* because we had given up the topics of Vaikuṇṭha and were engaged in discussing the topics of this material world (*ku-kathā*), our good spiritual values were completely destroyed. Our sensory, intuitive, reasoning, idealistic, and devotional values were expressed in a very simple sentence by you: “They love dog instead of God.”

But the power of serving your lotus feet revived our faith and our ability to appreciate the message of the Lord given to us in the scriptures. In his *Sārārthadarśinī* (a commentary on the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*) Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī writes this comment to *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.2.16:

The good fortune of receiving the mercy of an elevated pure devotee by rendering service to him results in the rekindling of one’s innate faith in the words of *sādhu*, guru, and *śāstra*. And by finding the shelter of a bona fide spiritual master, who is the sum and substance of all holy places of pilgrimage, the faithful devotee who desires to hear *kṛṣṇa-kathā* can awaken his taste for *kṛṣṇa-kathā* at that spiritual master’s lotus feet.

In *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.5.29, Śrī Nārada Muni describes the qualities of the soul completely surrendered to the personality of Godhead as *anuraktasya* (attached to the spiritual master), *praśritasya* (obedient), *śraddadhānasya* (having faith in the words of the spiritual master), and *anucarasya* (strictly following the instructions of the spiritual master). These qualities were the foundation on which you constructed this ISKCON movement. Already in 1958 you showed your mature vision as a *mahā-bhāgavata, uttama-adhikārī* devotee, a vision you wanted to make a reality in the near future. A few verses an essays of yours entitled Vṛndāvana Bhajan demonstrate this:

The Vaiṣṇavas can awaken the sleeping world;

By their mercy the sinful can all become devotees.

Therefore they do not perform *nirjana bhajana*;

That is the world-cheating process of the *kaniṣṭha-adhikārī*....

The *kaniṣṭha-adhikārīs* do not understand these scriptures.

In the false pride of being alone they simply hunt for *chapātis* and *dāl*.

Our spiritual master said these are all *kaniṣṭha.*

Now I can understand the truth of what he said.

One well versed in the scripture and who has strong faith

Is an *uttama-adhikārī* and can deliver the whole world.

He is the deliverer of the most fallen and is famous through the world.

Now, save this fallen one and thus become really famed.

And you in truth became glorious for an eternity. Your sun of glory will give light and spiritual warmth in the form of divine inspiration to all the future generations of sincere souls who traverse the path of pure devotional service.

Your glory was also predicted by Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī. You were always so eager to serve your Guru Mahārāja. Once in Bombay you were renting a little flat at Grand Road. One day when you walked on the street you met Śrīla Bhaktirakṣaka Śrīdhara Mahārāja and Śrīla Bhaktisāraṅga Gosvāmī Mahārāja, two Godbrother *sannyāsīs* from the Gauḍīya Maṭh. They told you that they’d been sent by Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī to start a temple. You helped them in many ways. The three of you went preaching every night. You used your contacts, set up the programs, introduced the *sannyāsīs* to important people, etc. And soon you had a beautiful temple. When Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī came to Bombay and install the Deities, Śrīla Bhaktisāraṅga Mahārāja told him, “Why is Abhay living separately? He should be president of this Bombay center.”

Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī replied, “It is better that he is living outside your company. He will do. When the time comes, he will do everything himself.”

Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura could see that you were empowered by the Transcendence. In your transcendental Bhaktivedanta purport to *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 9.10.20, you explain:

There are two kinds of strength—*daiva* and *puruṣākāra*. *Daiva* refers to the strength achieved from the Transcendence, and *puruṣākāra* refers to the strength organized by one’s own intelligence and power. Transcendental power is always superior to the power of the materialist.

The *Vedas,* which are described as the *setu,* or bridge, over which we can cross the ocean of material existence, teach us that in the conditioned stage one’s conceptions of life are polluted by passion and ignorance, which are exhibited by attachment, hostility, greed, illusion, madness, etc. All these are *śatravaḥ,* enemies. To conquer these enemies, Lord Kṛṣṇa gives us the sword of knowledge in the form of His instructions, which we receive trough His representative, the spiritual master. And if we sincerely serve guru and Kṛṣṇa, we will get from Lord Balarāma the power to wield this sword of knowledge, and then by their mercy we will be able to conquer the enemies mentioned above.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this auspicious day of your divine appearance, please bless us with the understanding that only when we accept your *daiva* instructions with all sincerity will we be able to get from the Lord the strength to fight the enemies of passion and ignorance. Then we will get steadiness and intelligence to maintain ISKCON as a pure spiritual organization, power to overcome our personal ambitions, and, most important, the inspiration to continue preaching Lord Caitanya’s teachings. Otherwise, our *puruṣākāra* strength leads only to disappointment, criticism, and quarrels, which are very dangerous for your movement.

Please bless us to remain sincere in our endeavors to serve you. May your transcendental battle drum always guide us on the path of pure devotional service, and let us play our little *karatālas* in your preaching band and be satisfied and proud of our position.

Your humble servants in the Kiev temple, Ukraine.  
(written by Prema Vānya dāsa)

## Kuala Lumpur, Malaysia

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

All glories to this most auspicious day of your divine appearance! Our respectful obeisances unto the dust of your lotus feet, which are the shelter of countless number of souls—past, present, and future.

May 1971 may not be of historical significance to many, but Your Divine Grace’s visit to Malaysia during that month blessed this country. Your Divine Grace planted the seeds of devotion in this soil, and in due course shoots sprouted out in the form of small devotee congregation.

Today ISKCON Malaysia is a young and healthy plant with ten centers, countless *nāma-haṭṭas,* and over 2,000 practicing devotees.

For many years ISKCON Kuala Lumpur has acted as the pace-setter in Malaysia, and today with renewed zeal and enthusiasm we are geared toward further exploring every possibility to expand your movement.

The new management structure of our temple has these goals:

Internally we want to establish closer co-operation between the congregation and the resident devotees, to improve individual devotional standards, and to establish the mood of love and trust which Your Divine Grace very much emphasized.

Externally we want to expand congregational preaching systematically, increase book distribution yearly, distribute more *prasādam,* and make our presence felt in the local community.

The devotees are also eagerly anticipating the renovation of our temple. Due to our insincerity and incompetence, the Lord has delayed the commencement of the work. We know the Lord will fulfill the request of his pure devotee. So, Your Divine Grace, if you feel we are qualified or sincere enough, kindly make this plea for us so that Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyāmasundara will manifest here in Kuala Lumpur to shower Their divine mercy on us.

Today, we renew our vows to do our utmost to work co-operatively in a loving atmosphere for your pleasure.

Thank you giving us your shelter.

Hare Kṛṣṇa.

Your insignificant servants at ISKCON Kuala Lumpur, Malaysia.  
(written by Uttama Caitanya dāsa)

## Kwazulu Natal, South Africa

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our must humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace and your ISKCON movement, which is extending your shelter throughout the whole world.

Here in Kwazulu Natal we are trying our best to fulfill your desires by maintaining our spiritual standards as you have given them, and by giving your message to others as much as we can. We are not very qualified, but by your mercy many nice things are happening.

To hold onto your lotus feet is the sure remedy for all the problems of Kali-yuga, individually and collectively. Others may claim to have solutions, but you have described such people as being like dogs trying to swim the Pacific Ocean, which is obviously a hopeless proposal. If someone catches hold of a dog’s tail, thinking he will thus be able to cross the ocean, that is even more hopeless.

In one conversation you said,

So this is the beginning, that if you want to be devotee, then you must approach the spiritual master who has got the power. Śrī-guru-caraṇa-padma, kevala-bhakati-sadma, bando mui sāvadhāna mate. Sāvadhāna means very carefully, not whimsically.

Sometimes when we are driving in India we come to some road works and see a sign saying *sāvadhāna,* or “warning.” We know we have to continue carefully; otherwise we may get into serious trouble. The path of spiritual life is often compared to walking on the edge of a razor blade, where a moment’s inattention will immediately lead to one being cut. However, if we remain fixed at your lotus feet we will be protected and will be able to make further progress on the path back to Godhead.

Unfortunately, Kali is trying to infiltrate your sacred movement in different ways, causing dissension among some of the members. You have told us that our love for you will be shown by how we co-operate together to keep your mission moving forward nicely. Here in ISKCON Kwazulu Natal, we are praying that you will help us maintain this mood of unity in your service, and help us to correct those who deviate, or if we cannot do that, to help us remain free from their influence.

Your servants at ISKCON Kwazulu Natal, South Africa.

## Lagos

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto my spiritual master, who has opened my eyes, which were blinded by the darkness of ignorance, with the torchlight of knowledge.”**

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet. Our respectful obeisances unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

The lotus feet of our spiritual master are the only way by which we can attain pure devotional service. I bow to his lotus feet with great awe and reverence. By his grace one can cross the ocean of material suffering and obtain the mercy of Kṛṣṇa.

In *Back to Godhead* magazine, Vol. 13, Nos. 1–2, a special issue published right after your disappearance in 1977, we find this wonderful statement about your glories:

Around the turn of the century, the great spiritual master Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura made this prediction: “Soon there will appear a personality who will preach the holy name of Hari \[Lord Kṛṣṇa\] all over the world.” As we have witnessed over the last twelve years, that personality was His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda. Throughout his life, Śrīla Prabhupāda prepared himself for his great mission, and from his arrival in America on September 18, 1965, to his passing on November 14, 1977—through some seventy books and thousands of letters, interviews, and lectures—he gave the world the holy names of Kṛṣṇa and the Vedic science of self-realization. Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura also wrote a short stanza about the influence that a Vaiṣṇava (a pure devotee of the Lord) carries in this world even after his passing:

He reasons ill who tells that Vaiṣṇavas die  
When thou art living still in sound!  
The Vaiṣṇavas die to live, and living try  
To spread the holy name around!

So, while it is natural for us to be sorrowful that Śrīla Prabhupāda has passed away, we can know for sure that he will never pass away, for he is “living still in sound.” As he himself said, “I will never die. I will live forever in my books.” In the days and years to come, may everyone come to remember Śrīla Prabhupāda in the same way that he remembered his spiritual master: “He lives forever by his divine instructions, and the follower lives with him.”

May this exquisite and grand Vyāsa-pūjā of our beloved, quintessentially great grandfather/mentor, His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, remind us of our indebtedness to him. Millions of lifetimes will not be sufficient to pay Śrīla Prabhupāda for his priceless blessings and mercies.

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, you gave everything to all and sundry. You gave humanity the essential ingredients for the regeneration of mankind, the fundamental aspect of human culture, the divine culture of antiquity. With your bold spiritual vision you built a house in which the whole world can live. Thus it is our wish here in your house (Śrī ISKCON) to contemplate your uncommon attributes:

**The Qualifications of the Spiritual Master**

\[Excerpts of\] an address given at the University of Stockholm in September of 1973 by His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda

In order to enter into spiritual life, two things are required. As enunciated by Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, one needs the mercy of the Supreme Lord and the mercy of the spiritual master:

*brahmāṇḍa bhramite kona bhāgyavān jīva*  
*guru-kṛṣṇa-prasāde pāya bhakti-latā-bīja*

**\[“The living entity wanders throughout different planets in different forms and bodies, but if by chance he comes in contact with a bona fide spiritual master, by the grace of the spiritual master he receives Lord Kṛṣṇa’s shelter, and his devotional life begins.”\]**

The spiritual master will never say, “I am God—I can give you mercy.” No. That is not a spiritual master—that is a bogus pretender. The spiritual master will say, “I am a servant of God; I have brought His mercy. Please take it and be satisfied.” This is the spiritual master’s business. He is just like a mailman. When a mailman delivers you some large amount of money, it is not his own money. The money is sent by someone else, but he honestly delivers it—“Sir, here is your money. Take it.” So you become very much satisfied with him, although it is not his money he is giving you. When you are in need and you get money from your father or someone else—brought by the mailman—you feel much satisfaction.

Similarly, we are all suffering in this blazing fire of material existence . But the spiritual master brings the message from the Supreme Lord and delivers it to you, and if you kindly accept it, then you’ll be satisfied. This is the business of the spiritual master.

*saṁsāra-dāvānala-līḍha-loka-*  
*trāṇāya kāruṇya-ghanāghanatvam*  
*prāptasya kalyāṇa-guṇārṇavasya*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

**\[“The spiritual master is receiving benediction from the ocean of mercy. Just as a cloud pours water on a forest fire to extinguish it, so the spiritual master delivers the materially afflicted world by extinguishing the blazing fire of material existence. I offer my respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of such a spiritual master.”\]**

\* \* \*

\[Excerpt from “The Gifts of His Divine Grace,” By Jayādvaita dāsa\]

The first point we might consider is that whatever Śrīla Prabhupāda did, he did not to serve any personal self-interest or create an individual charismatic mission, but rather to serve the will of the Supreme Lord, as expressed by the Lord Himself in the Vedic literature and handed down since time immemorial by the Vedic chain of teachers. In the recent era, Śrīla Prabhupāda’s spiritual master, Śrī Śrīmad Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, was the most outstanding representative of the Vedic line, and the spirit of full, unalloyed devotion to this great Vedic teacher and to the Supreme Lord Himself was the essence of all that Śrīla Prabhupāda did.

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, you personify the meaning of these words—theosophy, esotericism, theocracy, jurisprudence, pure *bhakti* and the personification of *sanātana-dharma.*

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, your transcendental appearance has stirred and humbled the world and made people more reflective and introspective, given them an opportunity to perfect their lives.

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your efforts the hippies became happies and saints, the flowers of the world, and ISKCON’s dancing white elephants.

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, under your umbrella are sheltered those in the states of despair, loneliness, amnesia, fear, and shock.

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have taught the world that it will not do any good to drive a car from the back seat.

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, for the sake of saving humanity, in your early days in America you took time to visit the Avalon Ballroom, courtesy of your benevolent son His Holiness Mukunda Goswami.

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, in those early difficult days in the sinful cities of America, your typewriter was stolen. You wandered the harsh and uncultured streets of New York City, a fearless warrior for Lord Caitanya, alone in difficult circumstances.

Our dearest Śrīla prabhupāda, you gave transcendental knowledge of God to college professors, celebrities, world leaders, philanthropists, military personnel, and even hardened demons. And you made the world to know that knowledge begins when one comes to know that the soul is separate from the body and pervades it with consciousness. You boldly defined the UN as an organization of confused, disunited animals bereft of real human culture.

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, as King Indradyumna succeeded in establishing Śrī Jagannātha in Orissa, you boldly made Śrī Jagannātha known all over the world.

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, your strength, positive thought, divine inspiration, enthusiasm, compassion, humility, love divine, dedication, wisdom, and philosophy are unparalleled and immeasurable. Even the great Ananta Śeṣa cannot comprehend your glories.

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, you will forever be known as the pioneer of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world, for you inaugurated Hare Kṛṣṇa communities all in the East, West, South, and North.

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, history has it that the meaning of “Abraham” of the Bible is “father of many nations.” In this modern era you single-handedly performed this feat. You are the father of many nations, for you built a house in which the people of all nations can live. This is an unparalleled feat.

Our dearest Śrīla prabhupāda, history also has it that Jesus Christ resurrected Lazarus from dead. Again, in this modern age, a destructive technological era fraught with forgetfulness, you single-handedly resurrected millions of lives. Despite our frail and feeble spiritual visions, you have cured lots of souls like us step by step. You created perfection from imperfection; you replaced impossibilities with possibilities.

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, on the order of your benevolent spiritual master, His Divine Grace Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī, you went around the world and preached the *bhakti* cult of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya, distributing the holy names of Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Śrīla Rāmānujācārya did it only in India, but you boldly took the holy names beyond India and made them known all over the world.

Our dearest Śrīla prabhupāda, you chanted the holy names of God at your departure from this physical plane to the spiritual world. In this way you gave us everything, even teaching us the science of how to die.

Dearest, dearest, and dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, just before your departure you called all your Godbrothers and begged them for forgiveness. They said, “No, no, you have committed no offense. You delivered so many from darkness and the state of amnesia and gave them light.”

Oh! Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, our quintessential father, in your Vedic books you reside, in the hearts of your followers you reside, in Vaikuṇṭhaloka you reside with the cowherd boys and girls, and with Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Again and again we declare, O dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, that you are the anchor that holds the boat of the universe and you are the true captain of this boat.

We have already crossed the threshold of our goals (in service to you), and there is a very limited time in which these goals can be efficiently reached. It would perhaps be purely and richly good for us to pause at this crucial movement for a while and to visualize the personalities and the sweet memories of our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda. And we beg every soul to do this very seriously and deliberately, in all ways to give overwhelming praise to our “grandfather His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda divine,” as our sweet mother Śrīmatī Aticandra would say. And if we consider everything honestly and devotionally, we realize that, finally without reservation, it is clear and obvious that we are forever indebted to our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda.

We see this Mahā-Vyāsa-pūjā as a continuation of Śrīla Prabhupāda’s centennial celebration. In fact, we see *every* day as part of our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda’s centennial celebration.

This critical history-making period should not be taken lightly. Let us work very hard to create a balanced, peaceful, Kṛṣṇa conscious world in this crucial period. Once again we express our heartfelt thanks to Śrī Kṛṣṇa for allowing us to experience this Mahā-Vyāsa-pūjā celebration.

Hare Kṛṣṇa. We remain  
Your insignificant servants in Lagos, Africa.

## Leicester, UK

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace, who are an ocean of compassion so deep and vast that we are unable to see its shore or behold its depth.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, I don’t know how to express my feelings of deepest gratitude for your having saved me, and not only me but the whole world, and not only now but for all time. Your mercy is transcendental to time and space and is therefore available at all times and at any place. Again and again I am deeply touched by the inconceivable depth of your compassion for such useless souls as myself. At the same time, I have an increasing and sincere desire to become a somewhat useful foot soldier in Lord Caitanya’s transcendental army under your command. I am also becoming more and more aware of how time is ticking by and how I have not really yet made any significant contribution to your great mission to save the world in its darkest hour.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, I am fully convinced that you will be pleased if we keep trying to embody even only a tiny fragment of your all-encompassing compassion by helping others to progress in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Seeing your compassion, we get a tiny glimpse of Lord Caitanya’s compassion. Hearing how your compassion has acted on the lives of so many of your disciples, we can begin to understand your eagerness to fulfill the order of your Guru Mahārāja by doing almost anything to attract and inspire all of us lost souls to go back home to Godhead.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, I will eternally treasure your glance at me in New Māyāpur in 1976. You looked into the bottom of my very heart and soul; your glance spoke to me, “Oh, you are also here?”; with your glance you eternally captured my heart. I therefore beg for the opportunity to serve you life after life, in whatever capacity you want me to.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, a few days ago I had a dream in which I was massaging you and serving you in other ways. It was a wonderful feeling to be so close to you. Please give me the opportunity to massage your transcendental body by distributing your infinite compassion to everyone without discrimination.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we ever forget what you have done for every single one of us? We are so unfortunate that we regard our insignificant differences as more important than your great mission of compassion. After all, there happen to be millions of people out there who are on the path to hell. What about them?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you never wasted any time. How much time do we waste with our ego battles? You said that our love for you would be shown by how much we co-operate after your physical departure. Now it is high time for us to prove whether we have really learned anything significant from you, whether we have somewhat matured over the years, and whether we have developed any real affection for you by putting your mission of compassion first. To save us you sacrificed everything in the service of your spiritual master. How much are we ready to sacrifice?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we deeply yearn for unity and harmony among all devotees. However, this we can achieve only by giving up our false pride and our desire for recognition, name, fame, glory, and respect, and by ultimately sacrificing our false egos in the fire of your pure compassion. This price we will have to pay, because real compassion does not come cheap. Are we ready for that?

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us the strength and maturity to again unite under the banner of your compassion. Let us work co-operatively, side by side, pushing back the tide of Kali. Let us remember your limitless compassion on all of us. You gave us this most glorious jewel of jewels. Let us jointly pass it on to others.

Your servants in Leicester, UK.  
(written by Gaurāṅga Sundara dāsa)

## Ljubljana, Slovenia

Dear respected Param Guru Śrīla A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda Ṭhākura,

Please accept our humble obeisances unto your lotus feet, which purified the whole planet as you preached the glories of Śrī Gaurāṅga’s and Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s *līlā*. All glories to Your Divine Grace, who are mercy personified!

On the occasion of your divine appearance, which was predicted by Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu Himself, we want to remember some of your glories, which are unlimited and unprecedented. Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s glories are unlimited, and even Lord Ananta cannot describe them fully. Similarly, the pastimes of the Lord’s pure devotees, being beyond the reach of the material energy, are also unlimited. We are very unqualified and cannot serve you properly, but we want to sincerely please you. This is our hope—that one day we will be able to get your mercy.

You are the powerful general (*senapati*) of Lord Gaurāṅga’s army who spread His teachings and mission throughout the whole world. You are the dear associate of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava, and the moon among the stars of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura’s disciples. Reading the different biographies of your Godbrothers, we have seen that all were special souls. But even in such a great assembly, you shine like thousands of suns, having taken full shelter of the teachings of your spiritual master. You are the destroyer of different speculative theories and have given us real knowledge. You have shown us that we are not this body, that we do not belong to this chaotic world, but that we are eternal spiritual souls and that our true business is to serve Kṛṣṇa in full bliss.

Many surrendered souls are following your instructions, having made your transcendental words the treasure of their lives. They are traveling around the world to serve you in your mission. Please make us their servants and protect us from the false followers, who bring troubles and deviations. Please protect us.

We have had some difficult times in our *yātrā,* but your mercy, which you give freely to the sincere souls, is keeping us alive. The spiritual master is very merciful to the disciples, and the spiritual grandfather is even more merciful. So we ask you, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, to give us the blessings and power to spread your preaching mission in Slovenia. This preaching mission you established is the only hope the fallen souls of the Age Kali have for crossing over the vast ocean of material existence. It is their only hope for getting some mercy and being saved from hellish conditions in future lives. We understand that we have been sleeping a little in the lap of the witch Māyā and have forgotten our real business—preaching. Now, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we want to be more useful tools in your hands, and we want to serve you sincerely with all our efforts.

The humble servants of your servants in Ljubljana, Slovenia.

## London (Soho)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances. All glories to you!

We are realizing more and more the significance of the legacy you have left us here in London. This is perhaps the greatest preaching opportunity on the planet, one so cherished by you and your Guru Mahārāja. You even left us with your most favorite Deities, so that we can take daily inspiration to carry out your mission: that everyone all over London will come to take *darśana* of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara, recognizing Them as the real proprietors, controllers, and enjoyers—as well as that best friend and infallible well-wisher for whom everyone is searching.

This is such a great task, there is really no time to think of anything else. Because there is so much to do. This is your great spiritual expertise, for in this way you are keeping us busy in Kṛṣṇa’s service. In this way, we can also be saved from engagement in Māyā’s domain. Who else but you could have done this for us?

So please continue to bless us in this way, as we know that this service is your real mercy and our genuine link to you and Lord Caitanya.

Always hoping to remain  
Your servants, the servants of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara at Soho St., London, UK.

## London (south)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your divine lotus feet, which are the source of great comfort and relief to your devotees. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, whose divine lotus feet are our shelter from the onslaught of Māyā. Please accept this offering from the unqualified devotees of your disciples, and excuse any impudent comments below.

**Impossible:** Over five hundred years ago, Lord Caitanya predicted that the holy names of Lord Kṛṣṇa would be chanted in every town and village all over the world. Many people were of the view that this prediction of Mahāprabhu’s was just an aphorism.

**Impossible:** In the 19thcentury Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura dreamt that one day people from countries all over the world, including Prussia, the UK, the USA, and other Western countries, would come together on the streets of Māyāpur to chant the holy names and dance.

**Impossible:** for a sixty-eight-year-old person (a week before his 69thbirthday) to leave the transcendental land of Vṛndāvana and travel alone by ship across the Atlantic to the USA, a place he had never been before, with simply two trunk-loads of books, a few personal possessions, and Rs40 (about US$10). There he winds up in New York’s Bowery (reputed to be one of the most degraded and corrupt places in the world and definitely “no place for a gentleman”) and starts a religious movement, which rapidly spreads throughout the world.

**Impossible:** to make the “hippies” (who were heavily into the American drug and sex culture) into “happies” (Kṛṣṇa conscious devotees).

**Impossible:** A person who had already suffered two heart attacks on his journey to the USA travels, in just twelve years (from 1966 to 1977), around the world fourteen times, establishing 108 Kṛṣṇa conscious temples (and *gurukulas,* farming communities, etc.) in practically every country of the world, initiates over 10,000 devotees into the glorious Brahma-Madhva-Gauḍīya-sampradāya, and inspires millions of people to chant and glorify the holy names of the Lord.

**Impossible:** that same personality writes, translates (from Sanskrit/Bengali to English), publishes, and inspires the distribution of over 70 volumes of holy books, including the *Bhagavad-gītā,* the 18,000-verse *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* and the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta*. These books have been translated into practically every language in the world, and millions of books have been distributed in every country.

**Impossible:** to establish magnificent temples in Māyāpur, Vṛndāvana, and Mumbai and inspire a city to be built with many temples in Māyāpur, which inspire all the devotees around the world.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are no ordinary man, and you always said that “impossible” is a word in a fool’s dictionary. You achieved all these “impossible” feats (and others) by your extraordinary demonstration of courage and determination to fulfill the desire of Lord Caitanya, Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura, and your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura Prabhupāda. Please bless these fools in South London to achieve the impossible and assist you in your extraordinary movement.

The servants of your servants in South London, UK.  
(written by Nābhinandana dāsa)

## Longdenville, Trinidad

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you on this most auspicious occasion of your Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā!

*vyavasāyātmikā buddhir ekeha kuru-nandana*  
*bahu-śākhā hy anantāś ca buddhayo ’vyavasāyinām*

**“Those who are on this path are resolute in purpose, and their aim is one. O beloved child of the Kurus, the intelligence of those who are irresolute is many-branched.” (*****Bhagavad-gītā*** **2.41)**

You once told your disciples that in the 1950’s, before you spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the world, you read the commentary on this verse written by Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura. You said you were struck by the commentary, which further fixed you in your resolve to follow Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura’s order to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness in English.

How well you followed his instruction! You never deviated in your determination. Beset by obstacles and disappointments, you appeared for many years to be unsuccessful. Yet you were undaunted, and your unswerving dedication to his order was your real success. As a result, you were rewarded with unparalleled potency to spread the mercy of Lord Caitanya.

Your success in pleasing your spiritual master has become our success and the success of the whole world too, for by your adherence to the order of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta you are freeing the whole world from the grip of Kali-yuga and delivering us to the highest plane of love of God.

Lord Caitanya describes you in his teachings to Sanātana Gosvāmī. “One who is expert in logic, argument, and the revealed scriptures and who has firm faith in Kṛṣṇa is classified as a topmost devotee. He can deliver the whole world.” (*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya* 22.65)

You point out in the beginning of your purport to *Bhagavad-gītā* 2.41that *vyavasāyātmikā buddhiḥ,* or resolute intelligence, means having firm faith in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. “*Śraddhā* is confident, firm faith that by rendering transcendental loving service to Kṛṣṇa one automatically performs all subsidiary activities. Such faith is favorable to the discharge of devotional service.” (*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya* 22.62) Thus faith and intelligence are synonymous.

Throughout your preaching you encouraged your followers to act on the same platform of resolute determination. You taught us to develop the mood Lord Caitanya expressed in His *Śikṣāṣṭaka*: “I know no one but Kṛṣṇa as my Lord, and He shall remain so even if He handles me roughly in His embrace or makes me brokenhearted by not being present before me. He is completely free to do anything and everything, for He is always my worshipful Lord unconditionally.”

Nonetheless, we may see that the reality of our faith does not meet the topmost standard. Too often we see that our intelligence is still on the platform of being many-branched. This is a dangerous platform, for the neophyte devotee can fall from the path of devotional service. Still, it is not a cause for despair. Lord Caitanya explains, “One whose faith is soft and pliable is called a neophyte, but by gradually following the process he will rise to the platform of a first-class devotee.” (*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya* 22.69) The development of our faith, you explain, is actually the process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness—a gradual one progressing from initial *śraddhā* to *niṣṭhā*.

What is the secret to becoming fixed in firm faith and thus resolute in our determination? The answer is in your purport to *Bhagavad-gītā* 2.41. “The resolute purpose of a person in Kṛṣṇa consciousness is based on knowledge....\[And\] to be well versed in Kṛṣṇa consciousness one has to act firmly and obey the representative of Kṛṣṇa, and one should accept the instruction of the bona fide spiritual master as one’s mission in life.”

Your life is the perfect demonstration of this answer. Simply studying your life should be sufficient inspiration. When we read in your biography of the difficulties you underwent for the greater part of your life, first in India and then in New York City, to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the English language, we become awed, humbled, and grateful. For the pleasure of guru and Kṛṣṇa you gave up your home in Śrī Vṛndāvana-dhāma and brought Vṛndāvana to the West. You were perfect and pure, you did not have to prove anything, yet you left the spiritual world to come to the hell of the Western world. Bearing the order of your spiritual master on your head, you went with him in your heart. “Because I am following his instructions,” you said, “I have never felt any separation.”

Still we fall short. We have the greatest gift, the holy names; the simplest and most sublime process to follow, Kṛṣṇa consciousness; and the kindest and most perfect pure devotee of the Lord, yourself, to whose order we can dedicate our lives. What more do we want?

In a purport to *Bhagavad-gītā* 2.66you write, “Disturbance is due to want of an ultimate goal.” We do not lack an ultimate goal, nor the means for attaining it, but because of the unsteadiness of our determination, our *bahu-śākhā* intelligence, we forget that the ultimate goal of our lives is the order of our spiritual master, and we create so many distractions and disturbances in our lives. But our lives are very short. You used to quote Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta to the effect that we should finish up our business in this lifetime and go back to Godhead.

The secret to how we can actually do this is in the society of devotees that you created for us to live among:

The spiritually powerful message of Godhead can be properly discussed only in a society of devotees, and it is greatly pleasing to hear in that association. If one hears from devotees, the way of transcendental experience quickly opens, and gradually one attains firm faith that in due course develops into attraction and devotion. (*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya* 22.86)

Alone we have no hope of attaining *vyavasāyātmikā buddhiḥ* and love for Kṛṣṇa. We are dependent on one another’s association. Those who have taken shelter of you as the expert captain for guiding us over the ocean of material existence have to sail in the ISKCON ship together, working together to serve your orders, helping one another, learning from one another, and learning to love one another. In this way we will fulfill one of your most important orders: that we should cooperate and in this way show our love for you.

Trying to do this has not proved to be as easy as perhaps we thought it would be. Surely it is only by your continued blessings on our collective and individual efforts that we will be able to overcome all the difficulties that have assailed ISKCON since your disappearance.

On this day, as we celebrate your glorious appearance in this world, we realize that we have something less to offer you than the *vyavasāyātmikā buddhiḥ* you expect of us. Thus we pray wholeheartedly for the firm faith and determination to remain following in your footsteps in the association of your dedicated followers, on the path you have pointed out to us, the one that leads back home, back to Godhead.

Your servants at the Śrī-Śrī-Rādhā-Gopīnātha Mandir, Longdenville, Trinidad.

## Madrid, Spain

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

In this most auspicious day of your appearance, the devotees from the Madrid temple and its congregation feel very proud of belonging to your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. We pray for your blessings to be able to properly understand and practice your instructions.

Daily we sing “*Jaya* Prabhupāda! *Jaya* Prabhupāda!” and it’s very pleasing and simple. When we meditate on your personality and how you engaged all your time and intelligence in the service of guru and Kṛṣṇa, and how you arranged every moment to teach the art of devotional service, and when we contemplate many other qualities of yours that show us the special personality that you are, we realize our inability to represent you properly. Please forgive our fault and all our shortcomings due to our materialistic nature, and please give us inspiration so that we remain loyal to Your Divine Grace and to the constant chanting of the holy name.

Thank you for giving us the opportunity of going back home, back to Godhead.

Your servants at ISKCON Madrid, Spain.  
(written by Jayānta dāsa)

## Manchester, UK

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Recently we have been researching your teachings on the duties of spiritual leaders within ISKCON. We found many interesting instructions, amongst which was this letter to one of your GBC’s:

You mention you like to speak now very often but the first business should be to preach to the devotees. It is better to maintain a devotee than try to convince others to become devotees. It is the duty of the GBC to *maintain* the devotees, keep them in the highest standard of Krishna Consciousness, and give them all good instruction, and let them go out and preach for making more devotees. Your first job should be to make sure that every one of the devotees in your zone of management is **reading our literatures and discussing the subject matter seriously from different angles of seeing**, and that they are somehow or other absorbing the knowledge of Krishna Consciousness philosophy. If they are fully educated in our philosophy and if they can get all of the knowledge and study it from every viewpoint, then very easily they will perform tapasya or renunciation and that will be their advancement in Krishna consciousness. \[Letter to Satsvarūpa dāsa Goswami, 16 June 1972 (my emphasis in bold)\]

This instruction to read and discuss your books from different angles of seeing is becoming increasingly our primary focus here in Manchester. It is confirmed to us throughout your books.

For example, the four seed verses of the *Gita*are 10.8–11. In 10.8, Kṛṣṇa says that the wise who know He is the Supreme engage in His devotional service and worship Him with all their hearts. Then in 10.9, Kṛṣṇa describes the activities of such devotional service:

The thoughts of My pure devotees dwell in Me, their lives are fully devoted to My service, and **they derive great satisfaction and bliss from always enlightening one another and conversing about Me**. \[my emphasis\]

How do devotees get their succor and happiness? They are always enlightening one another and conversing about Kṛṣṇa. In the purport to Bg. 9.1,you again emphasize the value of such discussion:

Discussion of Kṛṣṇa is very potent, and if a fortunate person has such association and tries to assimilate the knowledge, then he will surely make advancement toward spiritual realization.

As a result of such discussion we become attached to Kṛṣṇa and develop firm faith that simply by pleasing Kṛṣṇa we can achieve all perfection. In reciprocation, the Supreme Lord promises that He will personally guide us from within our hearts and destroy all our ignorance (see *Bg.*10.10–11).

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are personally experiencing the potency of such discussions. We came to preach in Manchester without any qualification. The burden of raising and maintaining a young family as well as having to maintain the financial and mental burden of a preaching project is such that sometimes we have feared it might destroy us. However, the more we take shelter of hearing and discussing your books systematically, the more we feel your reciprocation and protection.

There are three of us who have regularly been meeting for some time now, and recently others are joining us. We are discussing your purports threadbare, sentence by sentence. These sessions are like heart massages, and they are the basis of a growing mutual respect and love amongst us.

We are saddened when after many years of devotional service devotees experience difficulties. Sometimes they give up the process of *śravaṇam kīrtanam,* thinking it hasn’t helped solve the problems of their lives. Often, while continuing some commitment to Kṛṣṇa consciousness, they look to psychotherapy and other remedies to find solutions to the pressing problems of their lives. While I understand that one can find valuable pieces of wisdom in these other schools of thought (just as it is possible to find gold in a dirty place), the fact of the matter is that you have given us a mine of gold in the process of *śravaṇam kīrtanam*.

Unfortunately, this process sometimes seems to be relegated to the status of some dry ritual we undergo. We go to a *Bhāgavatam* class and hear someone speak for forty-five minutes, often as our own mind drifts, and after many years of such *śravaṇam kīrtanam,* we stop attending *Bhāgavatam* classes with the conviction that it is an irrelevance.

But our experience is that when we turn class into a participative experience of *saṅkīrtana* (complete hearing and chanting), where devotees are encouraged to share and understand one another’s realizations of your purports, then the classes become the most effective therapy for all the troubles of the mind.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you for giving us this solace. Your books are nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa Himself, and, like Him, if they are properly approached they are more soothing than millions of moons. We pray that this clumsy testimony of their potency may encourage others to set up their own discussion groups and find solace from the miseries of material existence.

Your aspiring servants at the ISKCON center in Manchester, UK.  
(written by Cintāmaṇi-dhāma-devī dāsī)

## Manila, the Philippines

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Kindly accept our most humble obeisances at your most divine lotus feet. All glories to your most auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā day!

As the sun appears at the break of dawn and dispels the covering of darkness, you have similarly come amongst us, opening our eyes from deep slumber to a life full of bliss and spiritual hope. Indeed, you are much more powerful than millions suns put together, because by your mere presence you have transformed and purified millions of fallen conditioned souls, making us eligible to engage in devotional service to Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Your glories are unlimited, and by your causeless mercy you allowed us to enter into your association although we are most unqualified. Without your compassion we are quite certain that we would still be under the strong clutches of *māyā*. What would the world be without your mercy? How could we even have begun to chant Hare Kṛṣṇa if you had not sacrificed for our sake? Would we ever have relished *prasādam* if it weren’t for the famous Sunday Love Feast you started? And would we ever have understood the mystery of man’s existence if you had not translated the Vedic *śāstras* into English? Without a doubt we would have been nothing and completely insignificant without you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

We continue to pray that you make us suitable and qualified menial servants at your lotus feet. We pray that we remain in ISKCON and faithfully serve the GBC and our spiritual masters with full enthusiasm and devotion. We also pray that your ISKCON will be protected from opposing forces and withstand the test of time. Kindly instill in our hearts what it takes to become mature devotees so that we can set aside personal differences and desires, thus paving the way to a stronger and better ISKCON family, where your disciples and grand-disciples co-exist in a harmonious and loving atmosphere.

All glories to your Vyāsa-pūjā!!  
All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!!

Your grand-disciples and aspiring grand-disciples at the ISKCON temple in Manila, the Philippines.  
(written by Śrīdāma dāsa)

## Manipur

Most worshipable Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

The year 2000 was a momentous one for your disciples, grand-disciples, followers, well-wishers, and, indeed, the entire state of Manipur.

The crowning event of the year was the Grand Inauguration, on the auspicious day of Kārttika Pūrṇimā, November 11, 2000, of the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Krishnachandra Manimandir, the centerpiece of the University of Bhāgavata Culture, under the auspices of ISKCON Manipur.

Manipur is known as “the Vṛndāvana of the East,” and the *bhāgavata* culture is deeply embedded in the consciousness of the local people. Recognizing this ancient spiritual culture of Manipur, we have chosen “*Satyaṁ paraṁ dhīmahi*” (“I meditate on the Absolute Truth”), a phrase from the first verse of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* as the motto for our university.

Unfortunately, in Manipur this ancient spiritual understanding of life is now being lost, especially among the youth. The task ahead of us, therefore, is to re-introduce the eternal, scientific principles of *bhāgavata* culture in a revolutionary manner. This alone, we feel, will bring true peace and happiness to all people, including the people of this state.

Towards this end, we organized the International Conference on the Culture of Peace as part of the elaborate opening ceremonies of the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Krishnachandra Manimandir. This peace conference, along with the March for Peace on the streets of Imphal and all the cultural and educational programs (lasting twenty-three days), was received extremely favorably by all the people of Manipur.

It is our fervent hope that our efforts at re-introducing the eternal principles of *bhāgavata* culture through the medium of the University of Bhāgavata Culture will bring true peace and happiness to the people of Manipur. Only when Manipur is manifested as a true Vaiṣṇava state, as you desired, will it be blessed with perfect happiness and peace. As Lord Kṛṣṇa says in the *Bhagavad-gītā* (2.66)

*nāsti buddhir ayuktasya na cāyuktasya bhāvanā*  
*na cābhāvayataḥ śāntir aśāntasya kutaḥ sukham*

**“One who is not connected with the Supreme \[in Kṛṣṇa consciousness\] can have neither transcendental intelligence nor a steady mind, without which there is no possibility of peace. And how can there be any happiness without peace?”**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this most auspicious day, please bless us so that we can humbly serve your divine mission.

Your sincere servants at ISKCON Manipur.

## Markham/Richmond Hill, Canada

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We humbly bow down at your lotus feet,

For it is there that we understood it is the Hare Kṛṣṇa mantra we must repeat.

We humbly thank you, Gurudev,  
For it is your mercy that you so generously gave.

We humbly bow down at your lotus feet,  
For without you and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, our lives would be incomplete.

We humbly thank you, Gurudev,  
For you showed us that to Māyā’s web we are not to cave.

We humbly bow down at your lotus feet,  
For you have us constantly chanting and dancing with the drum beat.

We humbly thank you, Gurudev,  
For you showed us it is Kṛṣṇa’s service that we should constantly crave.

We humbly bow down at your lotus feet,  
For you showed the world that Kṛṣṇa consciousness is not just for the elite.

We humbly thank you, Gurudev,  
For without you, it is certain we would never have been saved.

We humbly bow down at your lotus feet,  
For you showed us the proper way to live, perform ārati, and even eat.

We humbly thank you, Gurudev,  
For you showed us that it is Kṛṣṇa’s glories we should rave.

We humbly bow down at your lotus feet,  
For you taught us to refrain from fish, eggs, and meat.

We humbly thank you, Gurudev,  
For to bring us this knowledge and mercy, the ocean and sea you had to brave.

We humbly bow down at your lotus feet,  
For you built temples around the world starting with even pouring the concrete.

We humbly thank you, Gurudev,  
For without you, we would still be materialistic slaves.

We humbly bow down at your lotus feet,  
For you showed us how with Vedic philosophy Māyāvādīs will be defeat.

We humbly thank you, Gurudev,  
For the path to Godhead, you did pave.

We humbly bow down at your lotus feet,  
For you spread the holy name to every town, village, and street.

We humbly thank you, Gurudev,  
For you continue to shower us with mercy like a tidal wave.

We humbly bow down at your lotus feet,  
For you showed us that there is no living soul that we should mistreat.

We humbly thank you, Gurudev,  
For you were so kind that previous indiscretions you forgave.

We humbly bow down at your lotus feet,  
For you made Kṛṣṇa a household name and that was no easy feat.

We humbly thank you, Gurudev,  
For you showed us that treating all with respect is how we should behave.

We humbly bow down at your lotus feet,  
For you taught us that we should spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness and not live in retreat.

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda! *Jaya* Gurudeva!  
Your humble servants in Markham/Richmond Hill, Canada.  
(written by Mīnākṣī-devī dāsī)

## Mexico City, Mexico

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, our most beloved master and father,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

On this most auspicious day of your transcendental appearance, we reflect upon the day you appeared in our unfortunate lives and how our faces have always been decorated with the smile of Kṛṣṇa consciousness since that day.

Here in the Mexico Mandira, the devotees living in the temple and the congregational devotees are mostly your grand-disciples. We did not have the mercy of seeing your transcendental *vapu*. Still, when we see, bow down to, and chant to your *mūrti,* we know you are with us and feel your love.

Dear master, so beloved to Kṛṣṇa,

Please never take away our most beloved Śrī Śrī Rādhā–Madana-Gopāla, Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, and Śrī Śrī Śrī Jagannātha-Baladeva-Subhadrā Deities.  
Rescue your devotees who have gotten confused.  
**A**ccept our humble food-offerings.  
Benedict our families and *gṛhastha* duties with Kṛṣṇa consciousness.  
Hit out our desires to enjoy material life.  
Uphold your loving strictness toward us.  
Protect your *saṅkīrtana* devotees.  
Appear in the hearts of our leaders so that they can conduct your ISKCON.  
Distribute your mercy to those humble unseen devotees who perform supposed minor services.  
Appear in our hearts and never leave them.

We are not interested in seeing you in dreams or in having you appear physically before us. We hanker to grow old doing you *pūjā* and to die taking with us the love of our Godbrothers and -sisters, and thus yours as well.

We are not pure devotees; we have just a small sincere desire to serve you. This is what we have; it is not much, but please accept it.

Remaining as your aspiring servants,  
The devotees at the Śrī Śrī Radha-Madana-Gopāla Mandir, Mexico City, Mexico.

## Miami, Florida, USA

Very dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We were born in the darkness of ignorance, but you opened our eyes with the torchlight of knowledge. Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you and your sincere followers!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, even a hard-core materialist would be amazed at your accomplishments: you suffered heart attacks on the way over to America; you started from scratch, with no money and no contacts; you wrote so many books in so short a time at such an advanced age. In this way you transplanted the Vedic culture all over the world, completely transforming sinful living entities, changing their hearts and making them devotees.

*Guru-mukha-padma-vākya, cittete kariyā aikya:* “Our only wish is to have our consciousness purified by the words emanating from your lotus mouth.” We pray to be able to understand your instructions when we read your books and listen to your lectures. Once in Hyderabad you said,

The British Empire expanded by military strength, and we are expanding by Ratha-yātrā. We are expanding our empire simply by Ratha-yātrā....We introduced three chariots in big, big cities and conquered them....We are stronger than British Empire. Simply by Ratha-yātrā we are conquering, and actually that is being done. What strength have I got? Forty rupees beginning. Simply Hare Kṛṣṇa and Ratha-yātrā, that’s all. That’s a fact. \[Conversation, Hyderabad, 21 August 1976\]

Here in Miami we have three Ratha-yātrās every year, all in established parades. The Junior Orange Bowl Parade in Coral Gables and the King Mango Strut in Coconut Grove attract thousands of onlookers and are very exciting, but the best is the Three Kings Parade, the biggest in the state. Over 350,000 fortunate souls get the *darśana* of Lord Jagannātha, Lord Balarāma, and Śrīmatī Subhadrā Devī. It takes a full three hours to walk the parade route, and both sides of the street are jammed with people the whole way. They are happy to see us; they cheer and dance with us as the chariot passes. Who can estimate the mercy everyone receives?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are begging for your mercy and special blessings so that we can remember you, please you, and do what you want us to do, the way you want it done. You are the great five-star general in the personal army of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu, so please give us orders so that the standard of Kṛṣṇa consciousness may increase.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are feeling very grateful for all that you have given us; we can’t thank you enough. Please help us always feel your presence, and continue to engage us in devotional service at your lotus feet.

Your servants in the ISKCON temple in Miami, Florida, USA.  
(written by Nartaka-Gopāla-devī dāsī)

## Miskolc, Hungary

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your divine personality and your glorious appearance day!

Please accept our effort to glorify you from our fallen situation.
