We hope that in the future your Vyāsa-pūjā ceremony will be situated in our heart as an everyday celebration because we are following your teachings regularly. We would like to repay our debt to you by remembering you, serving you, and trying to satisfy you.

It is beyond our understanding how fortunate we are to be your followers. The most important thing for you is to save the fallen souls, and we would like to become instruments in your hands in this mission.

Please help us fulfill your desire, not only in words but with action also. We have no qualifications to glorify you, but we try to offer more and more services to help you in your mission.

Please give us your mercy so we may get closer and closer to the lotus feet of Lord Kṛṣṇa.

Your insignificant servants in Miskolc, Hungary.

## Murāri Sevaka farm, Tennessee, USA

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We grand disciples of yours at Murāri Sevaka offer our obeisances at your lotus feet. As you know, we have been through trials and tribulations, but now, under your worthy disciple His Grace Pṛthu Prabhu, we are back on track. Book distribution and preaching are vibrant again. Hence, please bless us with trust and faith in our Guru Mahārāja, so that we can humbly serve Their Lordships Nitāi-Gauracandra and continue distributing books in every town and village.

Jagat-guru, without your causeless mercy, we grandchildren of yours will vanish like straw in the stream of Kali-yuga. Hence please accept our prayers and bless us with unflinching faith in *guru-tattva*. You have created faith in the faithless all over the world, so please give shelter to your grandchildren at one corner of your divine lotus feet

Your servants at ISKCON’s Murāri Sevaka farm community in Tennessee, USA.

## Nagpur, India

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your divine lotus feet on this most glorious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā, the 105th anniversary of your appearance in this world.

We take this opportunity to express our heartfelt gratitude and appreciation unto you because on this day you mercifully appeared in this world to deliver fallen conditioned souls like us. You gave us the most priceless treasure, boundless love of Kṛṣṇa. Śrīla Prabhupāda, words are not enough to glorify you. We are eternally indebted to you, and we can never repay you for your unfathomable love, compassion, and kindness, but surely we would like to do whatever it takes to please you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you told us that we would please you by distributing your books. It is a joy for us to inform you that never in the history of Nagpur were so many books distributed as were distributed last year.

We are now a team of fifteen full-time devotees who plan to magnify your movement by working in cooperation as a unified preaching force.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you once expressed your desire to have a big center in central India by placing your finger on Nagpur on a map. We are putting in all our efforts dedicatedly to fulfill your desire.

On this blessed occasion we pray at the lotus feet of Lord Kṛṣṇa that He may bless us with the necessary wisdom and support to enhance and grow your mission—the mission of Lord Caitanya—in the best possible way.

Your loving grandchildren at ISKCON Nagpur, India.

## New Biharvan, Boise, Idaho, USA

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace! Thank you for giving us Kṛṣṇa consciousness!

It was during your Centennial year, in 1996, that we were inspired by you to build a new, bigger temple in Boise for Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Baṅkibihārī, Lord Jagannātha, Lord Baladeva, and Śrīmatī Subhadrā, and Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi. The temple opened in August of 1999. During the time the temple was being built and the two years since it opened, our appreciation for your glories has deepened. Each day we get newer and newer realizations—how you designed ISKCON to engage all of us in the five most important forms of devotional services described by Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī in *The Nectar of Devotion* and remembered by devotees as:

*Nām, Dhām,* Deity, Devotee, *Bhāgavatam*

After initial growing pains, the new temple is becoming a catalyst that allows the devotees, the congregation members, and the Boise community at large to take part in all of these devotional services:

**Nām:**We started the New Year with an eight-hour Japathon. Devotees and congregation members chanted 1.4 million names! You were with us, reminding us every so often to sit and chant properly. Every day our sixteen rounds are getting better, the majority of them being chanted before Śrṅgāra-ārati. *Saṅkīrtana* has increased. We regularly go to the university to distribute books. Through your grace, we have received permission to distribute books once a month at the shopping mall. Occasionally we also distribute at local parks and door to door. Devotees are giving classes in schools, colleges, and community education centers. Almost every other Sunday feast, teachers bring students from area schools to chant, dance, enjoy *prasādam,* and appreciate “Hare Kṛṣṇa” philosophy. Most of them are young, born after your departure from this planet. They are simply awestruck. Time after time, many of them say, “This is the best, most joyous religious service we have ever been to.” Every so often one or two in the group thinks that they know you and have seen you!

**Dhām:**Following your instructions, last year several devotees visited Māyāpur and Vṛndāvana, coming back revitalized and spiritualized. Several are making future plans to visit the holy *dhāmas*. Here, New Biharvan is also turning into a *dhāma* due to your presence. Visitors have told us that the peace and tranquility of the place amaze them. We continually hear how the temple is a spiritual sanctuary in Boise.

**Deity:** Our attraction for and service to “Boise’s Beautiful Baṅkibihārījī” increase every day. New well-fitted outfits, shining jewelry, and fresh garlands have enhanced Their beauty a thousand times in the last year, due to the increased love and services by devotees and congregation members. Cooking for Them is the most cherished activity. All our Sunday feasts are sponsored well in advance, and volunteering for cooking is the most sought-after service. Lord Jagannātha, Lord Baladeva, and Śrīmatī Subhadrā rode in a limousine to the Special Events Center to be welcomed by a packed audience. There They showered Their mercy upon the onlookers and witnessed an evening of dance, drama, and chanting put on for Their pleasure by the entire congregation. All if this has inspired Deity worship is several homes in Boise. There are at least four homes (one in each direction) where Gaura-Nitāi Deities are being worshiped. What is wonderful is that every one of the altars also has your “photo *vigraha*” on it.

**Devotee:**You often quoted this statement of Lord Śiva’s from the *Padma Purāṇa:* “My dear Pārvatī, there are different methods of worship, and out of all such methods the worship of the Supreme Person is considered to be the highest. But even higher than the worship of the Lord is the worship of the Lord’s devotees.” You have blessed us by sending many of your best disciples and grand-disciples so we can serve them. We know that, just like a benevolent grandfather, you are our well-wisher. The devotees appear when we need them the most. We know you are watching over us and making sure we do not go astray, for we see how you have also sent them for our *anartha-nivṛtti*—accelerated and instant purification—and to teach us many important lessons

**Bhāgavatam:**We have just finished rereading the *Pāda Padma—*the first two cantos of this desire tree of Vedic wisdom. We have indeed realized what we had heard, that you put everything there is to know in the purports of the first two cantos. In our Saturday morning Bhakti Shastri course, we have finished half of the *Bhagavad-gītā,* and we can witness the results of your powerful purifying purports as a Christian turns into a stronger Christian, a Buddhist/impersonalist into a personalist.

Our final realization is the one that’s the same year after year, except each year it hits deeper: that while a few of us, wrestling with all our egos, struggle to maintain and decorate a small ornament on the “Body ISKCON,” how you in your short stay on this planet were able to build the Body ISKCON and provide us with all the ornaments. But it should not surprise us. As you said, the world is surviving because of the mercy of a pure Vaiṣṇava.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your eternal mercy.

The devotees of New Biharvan, your parched grand-disciples in Boise, in the desert of Idaho, USA.  
(written by Anantarūpa dāsa and Aruddhā-devī dāsī)

## New Dvārakā, Los Angeles

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace on the occasion of your appearance day!

Your appearance day reminds us that you many times appeared here in Los Angeles. On your arrival, you would speak to the Los Angeles devotees, and many times you would say some special things about your Los Angeles center. On this day of your appearance, we, the survivors among your spiritual children and your new generation of spiritual grandchildren, find solace in remembering the days of your appearances in Los Angeles, and the words you spoke on those transcendental occasions. We can see that these words were meant not just for those present at that time but for all the future devotees who would come to find a place in the center that you created for their shelter here.

On June 29, 1971, you said in an arrival address at the Los Angeles temple: “So I am very happy to see you again after eight months, or nine months. *Ānandāmbudhi-vardhanam*. This movement is not hackneyed. Increasing the ocean of happiness.” And so you are happy today, to see new faces, new generations of devotees here in your temple. You are also pleased to see devotees who have been here for twenty or thirty years, finding ever-new happiness in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

You said:

That is the first benediction of chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa mantra, that our heart becomes cleansed. If your heart is not cleansed, how we are participating together? Somebody is Indian, somebody is American, somebody is Canadian, somebody is African. Because on the Kṛṣṇa consciousness platform the heart becomes cleansed. There are no more such consciousness that “I am this,” “I am that.” The only consciousness is that “I am Kṛṣṇa’s.” That is cleanliness of heart. As soon as we come to this platform, that “I am Kṛṣṇa’s”...Just like the gopīs. All the inhabitants of Vṛndāvana, they were thinking, “We are all Kṛṣṇa’s.” Central point is Kṛṣṇa. That is the beauty of Vṛndāvana. All the inhabitants of Vṛndāvana, the elderly cowherd men, the boys, the girls, the trees, the river, the birds, the beasts—everyone—they are simply thinking, “I am Kṛṣṇa’s.” This is Kṛṣṇa consciousness. And as soon as we think otherwise—“I am Indian,” “I am American,” “I am this,” “I am that,” that is material consciousness.

So New Dvārakā is a variegated temple, with devotees of all colors and nationalities. But we are working together for your pleasure, thinking “We are Kṛṣṇa’s.” You are absolutely right, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Without this consciousness, this Kṛṣṇa consciousness, we would not be cooperating so nicely together for your pleasure.

“Be Kṛṣṇa conscious,” you said. “How nice it is to become Kṛṣṇa conscious. We are living in...Not heaven. Heaven is nothing for us. We are living in Vaikuṇṭha in this temple. Anyone can practically see how they came from hellish life; now they are living in Vaikuṇṭha.”

We are very grateful to you for giving us this chance to live in Vaikuṇṭha. You continued,

So Kṛṣṇa consciousness is so nice and so simple. Why people are so foolish they do not take it? What is their complaint? We are living so nicely that sometimes people are envious, that “These people have no business, no occupation, and they are living in nice house, eating nice Kṛṣṇa prasādam, and chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa, and dancing!” They’re envious. But when we say, “Why don’t you come here?” they will deny: “No, sir. I cannot go.” \[laughter\] “Why you are envious? You come and participate and live like us, happily.” “No.” That he will not. \[laughter\] And this is the Māyā. You see? Māyā will say, “Oh, why you are going there? \[laughter\] You just come here...,and as soon as you get some money, you purchase one bottle and lie down.”

We can see that you wanted us to increase the population of your Vaikuṇṭha temple. You tell us there is no problem for maintenance: if we simply throw ourselves into the *saṅkīrtana* movement somehow or other, then everything will be provided, by Kṛṣṇa’s grace, just as in Vaikuṇṭha.

Therefore you encourage us to go out to the conditioned souls:

As living representative of Kṛṣṇa, our duty is to save these fallen conditioned souls. That is our duty. One may agree to come or not come. It doesn’t matter. You try your best to bring him to this Kṛṣṇa consciousness position. Then Kṛṣṇa will be happy. “Oh, this person is trying.” Because it is Kṛṣṇa’s business.

You have set up this ISKCON institution to carry out Kṛṣṇa’s business. And as members of this institution, employees of this transcendental business, each of us has the responsibility to be a living representative of Kṛṣṇa. All of us should try in some way or another to bring conditioned souls to the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa. They may agree or not agree, but if we make the attempt, then Kṛṣṇa will be happy to see us trying. Today in Los Angeles, we are, Śrīla Prabhupāda, trying in so many ways to please you and Kṛṣṇa in this way. Those who are doing this are happy and satisfied. Those who are not are unhappy and morose.

You continued,

We can serve Kṛṣṇa in four ways. First of all, try to dedicate your life for Kṛṣṇa. That is first class. If not, then spare some of your money for Kṛṣṇa. If not, then give some words for Kṛṣṇa. Or give some intelligence. You can give....You can serve Kṛṣṇa by your money.

On May 18, 1972 you appeared again in Los Angeles, at LAX. There you recited the *prema-dhvani* prayers, and ended by saying, “Los Angeles *bhakta-vṛnda kī jaya*!” Then later you said, “So my dear boys and girls, about six years ago I came to your country single-handed, with this pair of cymbals. Now you are so many chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa. That is my success.”

Yes, it is your success, and your success alone. No one but you came to deliver us, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You came, as you said, single-handed. You said,

It was the prediction of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu....Lord Caitanya desired that “In all the towns, in as many towns and villages as there are on the surface of the globe, My name will be broadcast.”...So His prediction will never go in vain. That’s a fact. So my plan was that “I shall go to America. America is the leading country of the world. If I can convince \[the\] younger generation of America, they will take up.” I am old man. I came here at the age of seventy years; now I am seventy-six. So my warning is already there. In nineteen hundred and seventy-one, I had a severe heart attack. You know, all. So the mission of Caitanya Mahāprabhu is now in your hands.

Yes, you warned us in 1972, and now your words have come to pass. The mission of Caitanya Mahāprabhu, as you established it, is now in our hands. And we see that some have left this mission as you established it. Some have fallen into *māyā,* some have developed loyalties to others, and some have concocted their own deviant philosophies. But some of us have remained, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We have accepted the mission, as you established it, and have taken it into our hands, and the same task you gave to us we now pass on to new generations of your followers. And there are no better words for their guidance than the words you uttered here in Los Angeles in 1972:

You are American boys and girls, very intelligent and graced by Kṛṣṇa. You are not poverty-stricken. You have enough resources, prestige. Everything materially, you are all well-equipped. If you kindly take this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement seriously, your country will be saved, and the whole world will be saved.

Then you said, “I was coming here, and I was seeing one film, cinema, “The Boyfriend.’” (Sometimes we also see these films on the airplane, Śrīla Prabhupāda, but perhaps not with your completely transcendental vision.) You said about this film,

Of course, young boy, young girl, making friendship, enjoying life—that is not condemned. Actually, that is life. Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa is young boy and young girl....This young boy and young girl trying to unite for happiness is the natural inclination of the living entity. It is there in the Supreme Personality of Godhead. In the *Vedānta-sūtra*it is said, janmādy asya yataḥ. Everything that we see in this material world is there in the spiritual world. *Janmādy asya yataḥ.* Wherefrom everything is coming? The original source, the original source of this love, is there in the Absolute. *Rādhā-kṛṣṇa-praṇaya-vikṛtiḥ.*It is the manifestation of the pleasure potency of the Supreme Personality of Godhead. So the thing as it is originally is perfect, but here, the same thing is only perverted reflection....This happiness is there which is factual: this Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa. You see Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa, how He’s jolly, with His cows, with His flute, with His comrades, with His boyfriend, girlfriend, how He’s happy....Here you are trying to enjoy the company of young girls, but Kṛṣṇa is also doing that in the spiritual world. *Sahasra-śata-sambhrama*, Lakṣmī. Each girl there is a goddess of fortune. Not only one, but many hundreds and thousands. They’re with great respect and reverence trying to serve Kṛṣṇa. *Lakṣmī-sahasra-śata-sambhrama-sevyamānam*. So you can also enter in the company of Kṛṣṇa, as Kṛṣṇa’s friend, as Kṛṣṇa’s servant, as Kṛṣṇa’s consort, as Kṛṣṇa’s father, anything. Everything...Here also, there are five relationships: relationship between master and servant, relationship between friend and friend, relationship between father and son, and relationship between boyfriend and girlfriend. All these relationships are emanating from Kṛṣṇa. *Akhila-rasāmṛta-sindhu*: the ocean of all pleasure. And Kṛṣṇa comes Himself to give you this message. Kṛṣṇa appeared five thousand years ago on this planet, and at Vṛndāvana He exhibited His real pastimes in the spiritual world, so just to give practical demonstration and to invite you there.

So you made it clear that the goal of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, as you established it here in Los Angeles and throughout the world in your International Society for Krishna Consciousness, is to bring each member back into the personal association of Kṛṣṇa in His eternal *līlā*. And we are confident that you have provided every resource and made every arrangement for this goal to be accomplished. There is no need to go anywhere else. Your teachings are complete and perfect.

But you went on to remind us that we should not focus simply on our personal spiritual development. You said,

Kṛṣṇa \[is\] canvassing: *sarva-dharmān parityajya mām ekaṁ śaraṇaṁ vraja*. So we have got this message from Kṛṣṇa, from Caitanya Mahāprabhu, from the six Gosvāmīs, later on, Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura, Bhaktisiddhānta Ṭhākura. And we are trying our bit also to distribute this knowledge. Now, tenth, eleventh, twelfth...My Guru Mahārāja is tenth from Caitanya Mahāprabhu, I am eleventh, you are the twelfth.

Yes, we your direct disciples are the twelfth. And it is our business to distribute the knowledge, following in your footsteps, by all means, up to and including making disciples in the line of disciplic succession. Those of us who have remained loyal to the vows we made to you at the time of initiation, those of us who have remained loyal to you alone as our spiritual guide, those of us who have remained loyal to the institution you established, are determined to take seriously the responsibility of being the twelfth. And now there is the thirteenth, the new generations of your grand-disciples who are now taking up the same responsibility, and who, before very long, will begin preparing the fourteenth generation of the followers of Lord Caitanya. Let them also remain loyal to their initiation vows, loyal to your manner of teaching and practicing Kṛṣṇa consciousness, loyal to the institutional arrangements that you have made for our spiritual progress.

So distribute this knowledge \[you implored on that day in Los Angeles\]. People are suffering. They are simply fighting on some false thing, *māyā*. They should be given the real fact of happiness....We are not sentimental, simply chanting and dancing. We have got more than two thousand volumes of books. If you want to learn it through science, philosophy, it is also there. Otherwise, the simple method—simply chant Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare, and you’ll realize the whole thing. The child can take part, the philosopher can take part, the scientist can take part, the politician can take part, the religionist can take part, the public can take part.

You regarded the Los Angeles temple as one of your world headquarters. So your vision for the devotees in Los Angeles extended beyond the limits of the city. You said to the Los Angeles devotees,

You American boys and girls are helping me so much, please take this philosophy, try to understand this philosophy. Make your life successful—your country and the whole world. You are helping other parts of the world who are not very much developed with money, resources. That is a very good idea. But in your country you do not see how the younger generation are going astray, how they’re baffled and confused. You should take care of this. The flower of your country, they are becoming hopeless, confused. Try to save them. And this is the only remedy, Kṛṣṇa consciousness, that can save your country and the whole world.

Today we are continuing in that same spirit. The devotees in Los Angeles are having an impact on their city, their country, and the entire world, and we promise to carry on in this tradition.

But you also warned the devotees in Los Angeles:

Don’t think because you are American, you have got material opulences, very comfortable life, cars, roads, buildings....That’s all right. But any moment, you will be kicked out, and it may be that you’ll have to accept another body which is not at all American. It may be the body of a tree or a cat or a dog. That science we must know. Don’t be enamored by the flickering happiness.

So over the years, we have seen that many of your followers have failed to heed your advice. They have become enamored by flickering happiness, and instead of assisting you in your mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the city, the country, and the world, they have become attracted to trying to acquire and enjoy material opulences without giving any of the fruits to Kṛṣṇa.

On April 13, 1973, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you arrived in Los Angeles again. You told the devotees you were glad to see them, and that you were especially happy to see that the Deities were being cared for nicely. You said, “Yes. I am very much thankful to you that Kṛṣṇa, Caitanya Mahāprabhu, came to your country. He wanted to come here, and you have well received and worship\[ed\] Him. Serve Him. He will be happy.” You knew, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that Lord Caitanya wanted to come here, and you engaged us in His service, and we wish to continue serving Him, according to your desire.

Again reminding us not to have a narrow outlook on things, you continued:

Introduce this movement all over your country, America is favored country. Little misguided about spiritual affairs, but if you introduce this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement in right way, keeping yourself to the standard, then people will accept it gradually.

So on this day of your appearance, we renew our pledge to you, to keep ourselves to the standard that you gave us, with the hope that more and more people will gradually come to take shelter at your lotus feet. You went on:

Kṛṣṇa is eternal, His pastimes are eternal. Just join with Kṛṣṇa, His rāsa dance, His play with cowherd boys, His dealing with His father and mother in Vṛndāvana. So our, this movement is to join Kṛṣṇa’s pastimes. *Tyaktvā dehaṁ punar janma, punar janma naiti mām eti kaunteya*. Simply if you try to understand Kṛṣṇa...That is not very difficult. Everything is explained in the *Bhagavad-gītā*, what is Kṛṣṇa. And further explained in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*. So if you simply read our books, follow the rules and regulations, chant sixteen rounds, it will be not very difficult. *Sevonmukhe hi jihvādau svayam eva sphuraty adaḥ*. Things will reveal to you.

Here once more you remind us that you are well aware that the purpose of this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, beyond giving the people of this world spiritual knowledge, is to bring us directly into the eternal *līlā* of Śrī Kṛṣṇa. And here you also indicate that you have given us everything necessary to allow us to fully understand Kṛṣṇa and enter into His *līlā*. You have explained everything, and you assure us that if we practice Kṛṣṇa consciousness exactly us you instructed, then we will easily achieve the supreme perfection. Everything will be automatically revealed to us as we follow your sublime teachings.

You said on that day in Los Angeles,

So if you, in this life, simply try to understand Kṛṣṇa, then your life is successful. What is that success? *Tyaktvā dehaṁ punar janma naiti*. After giving up this material body, no more acceptance of material body; we remain in our spiritual body. We have got our spiritual body. That is now covered by the material dress, subtle dress and gross dress. So if we give up the propensity for material sense gratification, that is *mukti*. So long we have a pinch, a drop of desire to enjoy material happiness, we’ll have to accept a body, different types of body, out of the 8,400,000. But if we become satisfied with service of Kṛṣṇa, then we have no more interest with this material happiness. That is *mukti.*

Let us always remembers these words of yours, and pray that the day will someday come when we will be satisfied with service to Kṛṣṇa, with no interest in material happiness. As you told the devotees on that day in Los Angeles,

If we say...to Kṛṣṇa, “Now, from this day, I surrender unto You. My Lord, I don’t want anything material,” then you are mukta, liberated. And if you keep the standard, māyā will not touch you. Just after giving up this body, you go back to home, back to Godhead. It is very easy.

So let us keep to the standard and go back to Godhead after giving up these bodies. But if we give up all thought of material success, and devote ourselves fully to Kṛṣṇa’s service, then won’t we be lacking something? You said, “Kṛṣṇa will give you all facility. He’ll give you facilities, whatever you want.” Of course, that means whatever we want for Kṛṣṇa’s service. You said,

So try to understand and be happy. Continue this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement in right earnest. So Kṛṣṇa will help you. *Teṣāṁ satata-yuktānāṁ bhajatāṁ prīti-pūrvakam/ dadāmi buddhi-yogaṁ tam*. Anyone who’s engaged with heart and soul in the service of the Lord, Kṛṣṇa will give him, everyone, He’ll give you full intelligence: “Do like this. Do like this.” Yena mām upayanti. He gives such intelligence by which you can go back to Him again. Thank you very much. We shall talk again.

So let us follow your instruction and continue this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement in right earnest, and in that way receive good guidance from Kṛṣṇa.

On July 8, 1974, you appeared again in Los Angeles. And on that occasion you made some remarks which are especially appropriate for your Vyāsa-pūjā day. After receiving the respectful worship of your followers, you said to them,

So I thank you very much for your kind reception. It is necessary.

*sākṣād-dharitvena samasta-śāstrair*  
*uktas tathā bhāvyata eva sadbhiḥ*  
*kintu prabhor yaḥ priya eva tasya*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

This is the process, Vedic process, to receive the transcendental knowledge through the *paramparā*system, and the reception or honor given to the spiritual master, it goes to Kṛṣṇa. Spiritual master is the official collector. Just like in government there is collector. He receives the money, taxes, from the citizens, not for his personal use but for the government. Similarly, this is the Vedic system, to receive knowledge through the transparent medium, guru, and to receive....Guru means the honor goes to Kṛṣṇa. So this is necessary. This is not an artificial thing, but spiritually it is necessary. Therefore we hold Vyāsa-pūjā day.

Yes, it is necessary, and we are gathered here today, convinced that this ceremony is not some artificial thing.

You then said,

So I am very glad to see that you are maintaining the temple standard as good as I saw when I left. That is my satisfaction. And keep this, I mean to say, situation, atmosphere, always, and follow the regulative principles, chant sixteen rounds.

Twenty-seven years later, we can tell you that we have maintained the temple standard as good as when you left. We hope you are satisfied. And although some of your disciples have fallen away, you can see that many of us old disciples and many, many new ones are here, following the regulative principles and chanting our sixteen rounds. And we hope this also satisfies you.

Not only do these things satisfy you and make you happy, they are also our only source of happiness. As you said,

You’ll remain always happy, because although we are in Los Angeles, we have nothing to do with the material atmosphere of Los Angeles. Therefore a devotee who lives with Kṛṣṇa or in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, he does not live in the material world. He always lives in the spiritual world.

On this day of your divine appearance in this world, we, your followers in Los Angeles, renew our promise to satisfy you by maintaining the temple standards and following the regulative principles and chanting our sixteen rounds.

You said, “So it is very simple method, and you are all intelligent boys and girls in the Western country, and I am very, happy that you have taken it seriously. And continue this.” We pledge to always continue our practice of the simple method you gave us and thus make you happy. And you said, “You’ll never be unhappy. And not only here but also . . .” But also where? In the spiritual world. You said,

*Man-manā bhava mad-bhakto mad-yājī māṁ namaskuru*....We are teaching these four principles in the temple—how to worship Kṛṣṇa, how to serve Kṛṣṇa, how to become Kṛṣṇa’s devotee, and how to think of Kṛṣṇa twenty-four hours. And if we do this, there is guarantee. Kṛṣṇa says, *mām eva eṣyasi asaṁśaya*: “Without any doubt, you will come to Me. You will go back to home, back to Godhead.”

“So do not be slackened,” you said. And we pray that we will never be slackened. You promised,

It is not difficult. *Su-sukhaṁ kartum avyayam*. This devotional service is so nice. It is...To execute the devotional is also very pleasing. *Su-sukhaṁ kartum avyayam*. And whatever you do, that is your permanent asset. It will never be lost. In the material world, suppose you have got some money. So that is not permanent. But anything, material possession, that is not permanent. Especially after death, everything is finished—another new chapter. That we do not know. But devotional service, it is guaranteed. It is permanent asset, even you cannot finish in this life. But that does not mean you should be neglectful. Try to finish the completely to become Kṛṣṇa conscious in this life. Do not keep in abeyance. That is not intelligence.

It is obvious you could see the future. You could see that your followers would be tested in your absence. And therefore you repeatedly said, “Do not be slackened...don’t be neglectful...finish completely...do not keep in abeyance.” That requires some determination, and we pray to you for that determination.

You said,

So execute this devotional service very sincerely, seriously. Even there is little inconvenience, tolerate it. There is no inconvenience. But if you think it is inconvenience—that is our mental concoction—still, you should not be neglectful in the discharging your duties in devotional service.

This instruction of yours is among your most important. For those who follow this instruction there is no obstacle in the execution of devotional service, no reason for discontent. If there is some personal inconvenience, we take it as the minimized reaction to our past sinful activities. If we see some difficulties in our Society or obstacles presented by outsiders, we take it as Kṛṣṇa’s mercy, another test for us to pass for your pleasure. Unfortunately, others meditate heavily on these ultimately illusory inconveniences, and victimized by their mental concoctions they become neglectful in discharging their duties and adhering to their vows. But in truth, there is no inconvenience.

And then you said to the devotees in Los Angeles,

So those who are engaged in Deity worship, those who are engaged in saṅkīrtana party, those who are engaged in distributing books, those who are engaged in the office for keeping accounts—and all of them in devotional service—not a single moment waste. *Kīrtanīyaḥ sadā hariḥ*. And whenever you have got time, read books. Now we have got volumes of books. Again we have got another five volumes of books. All are now immediately published. So you have got enough to study....The M.A., Ph.D. of the ordinary university will not be able to be compared with that. So rest assured that education, culture, happiness, satisfaction, and next, go to home, back to home, back to Godhead.

These instructions of yours fall like drops of nectar into our ears, where they transform into jewels that we guard carefully in our hearts. Each of us has some service. We vow not to waste a moment. Whenever we have some free time, let us read your books again and again and again. And let us be assured that we need look nowhere else for education, culture, happiness, and satisfaction in this life, and let us look forward to going back home, back to Godhead.

You appeared next in Los Angeles on February 9, 1975. Upon your arrival, you sang the song “Hari Hari Biphale.” You told the assembled devotees, “This movement is enlightening people because they’re simply wasting their time without worshiping Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa....This life is meant for reviving our lost Kṛṣṇa consciousness.” And then you assured the devotees, “There is Kṛṣṇa consciousness.” Yes, Kṛṣṇa consciousness is something real. It is the most sublime expression of religion, far surpassing what passes for religion in this world. Calling attention to the temple building, which used to be a church, you explained:

Otherwise how it is happening that this place was a church and nobody was coming, so much so that they had to sell it to others? We purchased it. When I first came here to see this church, there was nobody. The place is the same. The people is the same. I have not brought men from India. Why it is crowded now? This is the proof that in everyone’s heart Kṛṣṇa consciousness is there. *Nitya-siddha kṛṣṇa-bhakti sādhya kabhu naya.*

Your went on to say,

It is not artificial way of taking something sentimentally. Otherwise how in my absence all these boys and girls are maintaining the status quo very nicely? I am very glad. That is required. This is the proof that in everyone’s heart there is Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Yes, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us the actual key to spiritual life. You have uncovered Kṛṣṇa in our hearts, and if we stick to the process that you gave to us, Kṛṣṇa will remain uncovered. And in that way, even decades after your departure, in your absence we are still maintaining the status quo, and we hope this continues to engladden your heart. As you say, it is required. It is the proof of our Kṛṣṇa consciousness, of our Prabhupāda consciousness, that we maintain the status quo, that we maintain the integrity of your teachings, that we maintain the integrity of the Society you founded, that we maintain the worship of the Deities you installed, that we maintain the preaching programs that you initiated.

You said to the devotees in Los Angeles on that day,

People thought like that, that Kṛṣṇa consciousness is the monopoly business for the Indians or for the Hindus. They were thinking like that: “It cannot be preached. Nobody will take it.” But I thought, “If Kṛṣṇa is God, why it will not be taken by everyone? It must be taken.” So I was not very much hopeful to become successful. When I was in Boston port, I wrote one poetry that “I do not know why I have been brought here. How these people will take to Kṛṣṇa consciousness? As soon as I will say these four regulative principles, they will ask me to go away.” \[laughter\] It is very difficult for this country or this part of the world to follow the four principles of regulative life: no illicit sex, no gambling, no meat-eating, and no intoxication. Even Lord Zetland, when he was given this formula for becoming advanced in spiritual consciousness, he flatly replied, “It is impossible for us.” Yes. But impossible thing can be made possible by the grace of Kṛṣṇa. That is possible—Kṛṣṇa is all-powerful—provided we are serious and sincere. That is the main business. We must be very serious and sincere. Then Kṛṣṇa will give you intelligence.

We want to maintain your mood, Śrīla Prabhupāda, of doing the impossible. We know that every time we leave the temple to induce the conditioned souls in Los Angeles to become devotees of Kṛṣṇa, to get them to chant and follow the regulative principles, we are attempting something impossible. Even for ourselves, we sometimes think it impossible. But following your example, we take that step into the unknown, confident that Kṛṣṇa will make the impossible possible. And as soon as we try it, we see that it happens. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray for the strength to always keep ourselves in your mood of attempting the impossible. We pray to always remain serious and sincere, so that we can achieve the grace of Kṛṣṇa and thus please you by carrying forward your mission into the twenty-first century, with renewed enthusiasm and determination.

But the temptations of the material world are always there, attempting to rob us of our enthusiasm and determination. Therefore you said to the devotees in Los Angeles,

So this is the material world. You cannot satisfy anyone, neither you become satisfied, materially engaged. This is called material world. We may show some artificial satisfaction, but there cannot be satisfaction in the material world. First of all, you have to take it as axiomatic truth that there is no happiness and there cannot be any satisfaction in this material world. Then you’ll make, spiritually advance. If you have got little faith still that “I can be satisfied; I can be happy materially,” then that is Māyā’s influence. That is Māyā’s influence. There is no possibility. So if you want satisfaction, then you have to take to Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Compulsory: “You must.” There is no other alternative.

We pray for the conviction to avoid the false idea that we can be happy by striving for some kind of material happiness or satisfaction.

The decision to follow Kṛṣṇa or Māyā is, however, ultimately ours to make. You said,

You have to submit. Therefore Kṛṣṇa says, *sarva-dharmān parityajya mām ekaṁ śaraṇaṁ vraja*. That is the way. You have to surrender. So long you don’t surrender, that is up to you. Kṛṣṇa will not force you. He is almighty, He can force you, but He does not do that. He says, “You do this. If you do not do it, that is your business. But I say you do it.”

You also said to the devotees in Los Angeles on that day,

I am very glad that you are following despite all impediments. The Deity is nicely decorated, and the Deity is worshiped, and He’s pleased upon you. Be blessed. Continue this process, don’t deviate, strictly follow the regulative principle, chant Hare Kṛṣṇa at least sixteen rounds, and you’ll be happy. There is no doubt about it. You can experience. It is not an imposition. Don’t leave this process. Then, in this life, you will be able to understand Kṛṣṇa.

In these words, you gave us the keys to success in our spiritual life, our passport to enter Kṛṣṇa’s eternal *līlā*. We must follow despite all impediments. Impediments will always be there in this material world, but despite them we should follow and this will make you glad. We have seen that many have given up their practice of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the face of impediments, many have given up their loyalty to you and the Society you have created. If you were here today with us (and in one sense you are always with us) you would be happy to see that the Deity is still nicely decorated and worshiped, and therefore we are confident He is pleased upon us and that your blessings are still with us. You told the Los Angeles devotees to continue the process, so we are continuing, even though some have given up. You told the Los Angeles devotees not to deviate. Although some have deviated, disobeying your instructions to not go outside the institution you created, not to go to others for instruction, we here remain loyal to you and your teachings and your institution. You told the devotees in Los Angeles to strictly follow the regulative principles and to chant at least sixteen rounds, and we continue to accept these as the foundation for our spiritual lives in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You told the devotees in Los Angeles not to leave this process, and we here are not leaving it, although many have. We are confident that by following the process you gave us, with no additions or subtractions from anyone else, we can and will achieve the final goal of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. As you yourself said on that day, if we follow the process you have given us, this will “make you fit for being liberated even in this life.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your next appearance in Los Angeles during your manifest presence was on June 20, 1975. On the day of your arrival, after *kīrtana* you chanted, “New Dvārakā-dhāma *kī jaya*! New Dvārakā-dhāma *kī jaya*!” There was a little silence, and you asked your secretary, “So I shall go now? Speaking something? Our speaking is the same, *harer nāma harer nāma harer nāmaiva kevalam*.” The excited devotees replied, “Haribol! Prabhupāda!” And then you said,

We have no new discovery. We don’t manufacture. This is our process. We simply follow the predecessor’s instruction. That’s all. Our movement is very easy because we haven’t got to manufacture something. We simply repeat the words and the instruction given by the predecessor.

In these days, when many are trying to discover or manufacture something new, your words are a healthy reminder. You reminded the devotees in Los Angeles,

You are singing daily, *guru-mukha-padma-vākya, cittete koriyā aikya, ār nā koriho mane āśā.*Very simple thing. We are receiving the transcendental knowledge through *guru-paramparā* succession. So we have to simply take instruction from guru, and if we execute that to our heart and soul, that is success. That is practical.

You went on to say,

I have no personal qualification, but I simply tried to satisfy my guru. That’s all. My Guru Mahārāja asked me that “If you get some money, you print books.” So there was a private meeting, talking, some of my important Godbrothers also there. It was in Rādhā-kuṇḍa. So Guru Mahārāja was speaking to me that “Since we have got this Bagh Bazaar marble temple, there has been so many dissensions, and everyone thinking who will occupy this room or that room, that room. I wish, therefore, to sell this temple and the marble and print some books.” Yes. So I took up this from his mouth, that he is very fond of books. And he told me personally that “If you get some money, print books.” Therefore I am stressing on this point: “Where is book? Where is book? Where is book?” So kindly help me. This is my request. Print as many books in as many languages and distribute throughout the whole world. Then Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement will automatically increase.

Twenty-six years after you spoke these words, the Los Angeles devotees are still dedicating themselves to your desire that your books be printed and distributed in massive numbers. We remain convinced, as you were, that if we continue to print and distribute books, the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement will automatically increase.

You ended your talk by calling the devotees to enter the Vṛndāvana mood:

That is the Vṛndāvana picture. Vṛndāvana, everything is attracted with Kṛṣṇa. That is Vṛndāvana. The animals, the trees, the flowers, the land, and the cowherd boys, the elderly cowherds men, Nanda Mahārāja and others—all are attracted to Kṛṣṇa. This is Vṛndāvana. The Yamunā water, everything. Fruits, flowers, and everything. That is Vṛndāvana. So Kṛṣṇa is the center of attraction....Our position is, natural position is to be attracted by Kṛṣṇa. If we do not become attracted by Kṛṣṇa, that is unnatural. And to become attracted by Kṛṣṇa, that is not unnatural. That is natural. So we want to revive our natural position, uncovered position. Now we are covered by dust, by dirt, by corroding materials. So we have to cleanse this. Therefore the *bhakti* process is *sarvopādhi-vinirmuktam*....When we become cleansed of all dirty things of material modes of nature, then we are prepared to serve Kṛṣṇa....So this process, the *śravaṇaṁ kīrtanam*, this is the process of cleansing. So more you chant, more you hear, the more you become cleansed, more you become Kṛṣṇa conscious. Just prolong this process and be happy.

On this day of your appearance, we thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us this process that will make us eternally happy.

Your servants in New Dvārakā, your ISKCON temple in Los Angeles, California, USA.  
(written by Drutakarmā dāsa)

## New Ekacakra, Ekaterinburg, Russia

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this auspicious day of our appearance I bow down unto your lotus feet in veneration. I don’t feel myself able to glorify you, and I know that I am far from comprehending your true grandeur. I beg Kṛṣṇa’s mercy to realize your exalted position and in this way attain the fortune of my imperfect life. With timidity and embarrassment, for the first time during ten years of my service in your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, by the mercy of the devotees I was asked to write an offering commemorating the day of your divine appearance.

I have no doubt, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, that you are a very intimate and dear servant of Lord Caitanya. Recently my spiritual master, your sincere servant Jayapatāka Swami, recalled in a lecture that once one of the organizers of a spiritual festival said of you after your preaching, “In his heart Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu presents Himself.” You smiled, without negation. That is why I have no doubt that your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is a movement of Lord Caitanya, a movement whose goal is to elevate the most fallen entities to the most exalted position of *prema-bhakti*. In my short period of participation in your movement I have seen many incontestable proofs that this movement is the purest movement, supported and inspired by the Supreme Personality of Godhead and by you personally.

Yes! I am convinced again and again. You are alive, Śrīla Prabhupāda! Moreover you are the most alive person in this movement, because you personally support your sincere followers. I remember an article written by of one of your sincere disciples, who frankly declared that you continue to lead and support ISKCON. It written was during a hard time for the devotees, soon after you left this world, when many devotees left their active services and felt helpless. On his own initiative he printed a booklet with a devotee’s poems about Kṛṣṇa. You came to him in a dream and a spoke these simple words with great humility: “Thank you very much.” This single moment was enough to convince him that you were nearby and that you were still accepting the service of your disciples, and inspiring and leading them.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, today your movement continues to spread the mercy of Lord Gaurāṅga all over the world via the distribution of books, *prasādam,* and the chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra*. I am happy to take part in your movement and happy to serve you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. I am very thankful to you for all the causeless mercy you have poured on such a fallen soul as me. You saved me and took care of me in my time of difficulties through your sincere servants, and you mystically came to me personally in the form of your *mūrti*. For two years I have been worshiping you through your *mūrti,* and I have found this worship to be a safe shelter. So it is a fact that you save me every day, engaging my consciousness in service of Your Divine Grace.

But my dream is not only to bathe your body, to dress you in clean cloth, to offer you *prasādam* and paraphernalia for *pūjā,* although this activities are blissful. My dream is to become really useful to your mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness. To realize this dream, I fully depend on your mercy. On this auspicious day I am begging you, with the strongest desire my fallen heart is able to muster, to let me be a true servant of your servants in preaching the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Please test me. I will try to pass your tests and try to satisfy you. Although I am fallen and have no good qualities, I have a faith that “by the mercy of the spiritual master, a mute can become an orator and a lame man can cross mountains.”

Being *jagad-guru,* Śrīla Prabhupāda, you can fill all three worlds with the nectar of *saṅkīrtana*. So it is sure that a drop of your blessings can fill the life of an insignificant soul like me with the essence—the desire to preach the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Today and always I remain near your lotus feet, Śrīla Prabhupāda, in eternal expectation of your mercy. I need no other shelter. May everything in my life be as you allow:

*karuṇā nā hoile, kāṇḍiyā kāṇḍiyā, prāṇa nā rākhibo āra*

Your servants in New Ekacakra, Ekaterinburg, Russia.  
(written by Vasuśreṣṭha dāsa)

## New Gokula-dhāma (Austin)

My Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

On this most glorious occasion of your appearance, I wish to offer my humble *daṇḍavats* unlimitedly in the dust of your lotus feet.

These two lotus feet are the transcendental shelter for all the conditioned souls in this world. Anyone who takes shelter of these two lotus feet has nothing to regret in this material world. There is only gain and no pain.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your appearance in this world is just to reclaim the conditioned souls back home, back to Godhead. Thus your teachings are widely distributed throughout the world. Your potency is spread so widely that even though you have physically left the planet it is still acting like a touchstone. Anyone who comes in contact with these sublime teachings and practices them diligently can be freed of all material anxieties and become blissfully situated in full knowledge and love of Godhead

Although many may see apparent problems in your movement, a genuine seeker of love of Godhead will still find the truth within it. The transcendental shelter of your two lotus feet truly fulfills the purport of your transcendental name.

In 1971 you instructed your disciples to “make our Austin center a grand success.” Please bless us so that we may fulfill your desire.

Your humble servants at New Gokula-dhāma in Austin, Texas, USA.  
(written by Viṣṇupriyā-devī dāsī)

## New Gokula-dhāma (Vancouver)

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your wonderful lotus feet. Please accept these words I have written for this most auspicious occasion of the anniversary of your appearance.

In the early morning hours at ISKCON Vancouver, I was in Maṅgala-ārati, appreciating my *darśana* of Śrī Śrī Rādhā–Madana-mohana and relishing my association with the family of devotees, all of us chanting and dancing together.

Just another day in the progress and development of our Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

But wait—if it weren’t for you, this “ordinary” scene would not be taking place. No devotees. No Deities. No worship. No dancing or chanting. No kind of *sādhana* at all for us.

I was startled to appreciate that far from being ordinary, it was simply an extraordinary day in the midst of extraordinary journeys in extraordinary lives.

Our lives have been transformed by your touch, your care, your love, your words, your example, your dedication and service to the words of *your* Guru Mahārāja—indeed, we have been transformed by the causeless mercy of Your Divine Grace.

I hear in my mind the words you wrote at Commonwealth Pier in Boston upon your arrival on September 18, 1965: “O Lord, I am like a puppet in Your hands, so if You have brought me here to dance, then make me dance, make me dance, O Lord, make me dance as You like.” We can only fall on the floor in thankfulness that you took that dreadful journey for us.

Our dear father, Śrīla Prabhupāda, as your sons and daughters, grandsons and granddaughters, we beg that you pass this benediction to us: make us dance, make us dance, make us dance as you like.

Thank you, over and over, from the deepest recesses of our hearts, thank you for your kindness in coming and making our ordinary days most extraordinary.

The devotees of New Gokula-dhāma, Vancouver, Canada.  
(written by Madhumatī-devī dāsī)

## New Goloka, Hillsborough, North Carolina, USA

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! All glories to Lord Nityānanda and Lord Caitanya! All glories to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Golokānanda.

This year, by your mercy, I was able to visit Vṛndāvana during the month of Kārttika. Upon entering your *samādhi,* I was able to feel your presence very strongly. Listening to the guests chanting the *mahā-mantra* as they followed the lotus path on the top floor, I could understand how you are always engaging the conditioned souls in the chanting of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s name. Previously I had heard that one could not buy a ticket to Vṛndāvana, but that one needed the mercy of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī to go there. During my stay in the holy *dhāma,* I could deeply understand how I needed your guidance and protection to enter into such a spiritual abode. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our spiritual eyes. Without your vision, our eyes are covered by material conceptions of life. What can we possibly know of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa? You are so kind that, within your books, you have given us the proper direction to follow that will lead us back to Godhead.

On the anniversary of your disappearance day in Vṛndāvana, so many of your disciples spoke of their days with you. The room was full of their love and devotion. You are our eternal father, uniting us in Lord Kṛṣṇa’s service. On that day I could understand that, although we were feeling the pain of separation from your *vapu* form, you are always present with us in your *vāṇī*. I was praying that we can always keep the right mood and follow in your footsteps. Whether young or old, we must all work together to carry on this *saṅkīrtana* movement.

While in Vṛndāvana I had left behind all of my managerial duties in order to focus exclusively on increasing my hearing and chanting about Lord Kṛṣṇa. I was praying to become purified so that I could carry out my service nicely and be pleasing to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Therefore, when the time came to leave, I was very sad. I had become very attached to the Krishna-Balarām Mandir and to daily visiting your *samādhi* to chant. I was also relishing my visits to you in the Rādhā-Dāmodara temple, where you eternally reside in your quarters. How easy it is to think of Lord Kṛṣṇa on Govardhana Hill or Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī at Rādhā-kuṇḍa. So, as the day approached for me to leave Vṛndāvana, I went to see you in your *samādhi,* and I was crying.

When I looked at you, I felt that you were compassionate; at the same time, I could see that you were chastising me. You had sacrificed so much to come to the West and preach so that people like myself could learn about the glories of Lord Caitanya and the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra*. By your example you had shown us that wherever the pure devotee resides, that place is nondifferent than Vṛndāvana. I could understand that you were telling me that it wasn’t time for me to retire, but that I must return to my service and make sure that at New Goloka there is always Vṛndāvana/Kṛṣṇa consciousness. So I understood that, although it was so painful to leave, if I follow your instructions I will never be separated from you or Vṛndāvana-dhāma.

Now that I am back in North Carolina, I can see that there are many wonderful devotees here who are very faithful and dedicated to your mission. Please give us the strength to always take shelter of the holy names and to give up all petty differences. Help us unite to increase our preaching efforts and bring more and more souls to the shelter of your lotus feet. Thank you for going through so much trouble to bring this wonderful knowledge of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the West. You have shown us that the real Vṛndāvana mood consists of just wanting to give pleasure to Kṛṣṇa.

We hope you are pleased with our services here.

Your servants at New Goloka-dhāma, Hillsborough, North Carolina, USA.  
(written by Kṛṣṇapriyā-devī dāsī)

## New Govardhana, San Diego, California, USA

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

To be able to glorify you is a great honor and pleasure. Every Vyāsa-pūjā is like a mile marker on the path of our journey back home, back to Godhead, because on this day especially we take the time to reflect on how everything in our “world” of Kṛṣṇa consciousness has been given to us by you, how we are maintained by you, and how our further advancement in spiritual life is dependent on your mercy and our pleasing you.

Recently a devotee was telling me how he could no longer feel your presence in your books or followers and therefore it was necessary to find someone else to take shelter of. He was saying that he had to find a living guru. In other words, you were no longer “alive” in your words, followers, and movement.

As soon as I heard these sad words, I thought of your instructions about *vāṇī* and *vapu*. You stressed again and again that physical presence was sometimes appreciable and sometimes not, but *vāṇī* continues eternally. Therefore we must take advantage of the *vāṇī* of the spiritual master, not the physical presence. Even when you were physically present, how many of us could take advantage of that? You were constantly traveling, spreading the movement—but we felt your presence.

You told us that you felt that Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta was always seeing your activities and guiding you from within, since the Paramātmā is in the heart along with all His associates and devotees. You told us that you would live forever in your books and that if anyone wanted to know you all they had to do was to read your books. How many thousands upon thousands of souls met you through your books and thus began their Kṛṣṇa consciousness? You gave the example that although Kṛṣṇa is no longer present from the materialistic point of view, the *Bhagavad-gītā* continues.

From the very beginning of my Kṛṣṇa consciousness over twenty-five years ago, I have always felt your presence in your sincere followers, your divine words, and in the devotional service that you gave us to perform.

I feel your presence in your instructions, in my service to you, in your followers, and in your movement more and more each day.

On this auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā Day I pray that all of us will feel your presence more and more as our devotional lives continue. I pray that you remove whatever impediments in our hearts block this realization. I pray that you show special mercy to those who, for whatever reasons, feel they must take the great risk of going elsewhere because they no longer feel your presence.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, with straws of humility in my mouth and tears of gratitude in my eyes, I prostrate at your blessed feet. Please accept whatever insignificant service I can render and help me increase my service and my Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Your disciples, devotees, and followers in New Govardhana-dhāma, San Diego, California, USA.  
(written by Dharmasetu dāsa)

## New Guṇḍicā, Boston, USA

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept our humble obeisances.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this most joyous and sacred anniversary of your sacred appearance in this world, we, your followers in Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīvallabha’s temple in Boston wish to offer you our very best heartfelt appreciations. On this day we remember and feel grateful for all your glorious contributions, especially and specifically your giving Kṛṣṇa consciousness to Boston, a most important university center for the United States and the whole world.

In 1967 you sent Satsvarūpa dāsa to open the first Boston temple. He located a small storefront on Glenville Avenue, in a residential neighborhood near Boston University. When he entered the storefront, even before the devotees had moved in, he miraculously and auspiciously found lying on the floor a blue aerogramme mailed by you from India. The aerogramme said,

I can understand that you have secured a very nice place in Boston and there is very good possibility of pushing our movement amongst the students community there. Our movement is certainly very much appealing to the younger section of your country and if we are successful in the matter of attracting the students community in your country certainly this movement will scatter all over the world and the foretelling of Lord Caitanya that in every village and every town of the world the \[Lord\] will be famous for His glorious Sankirtana movement \[will come true\]. Please try for this \[with all\] your heart and soul and your life will be a successful mission.

Youth is the most wonderful time of human life. Certainly the real reason it is so is because of the easy parallel or connection to the full wonders of eternal spiritual youthhood of Kṛṣṇa’s pastimes in the spiritual world, Goloka Vṛndāvana. The innocence, idealism, beauty, strength, intense desire, and emotion that characterize youth all have their origin and perfection in a pure devotional relationship with Kṛṣṇa, in one’s desired mood, or *bhāva*.

Not only can a selfless, compassionate devotee who preaches on behalf of Lord Caitanya see the parallel between the material and the spiritual energies, but he can bridge the gap by connecting the temporary fleeting youthhood of forgetful conditioned souls to the eternal youthhood of *kṛṣṇa-līlā* through the power and grace of the *saṅkīrtana* movement. Thus he helps others become eternally youthful.

What you practically and unprecedentedly demonstrated in attracting the young people in America, who you called the “flowers of your country,” was expressed in the most powerful and pristine way as you saw America for the first time when you landed at Boston Harbor in September 1965. Your poem *Mārkine Bhāgavata Dharma* (“Preaching Kṛṣṇa Consciousness in America”) is filled with your intense mood of love and compassion. All the hope of lifetimes of the previous *ācāryas,* and your own life’s effort as well, reached its climax when you landed in Boston to begin the long-awaited mission of Lord Caitanya. You were fresh from the holy abode of Śrī Vṛndāvana-dhāma, and your first *darśana* of the Boston city skyline provoked powerful spiritual emotions in you, just as the first *darśana* of the Lord in the morning, newly dressed and decorated at the “greeting the Deities,” produces powerful first impressions and emotions that are not available at other times of the day, so much so that devotees call greeting the Deities “the barometer of spiritual life.”So in the same way the heart’s expression of your confidential desires and spiritual emotions that began and expanded the worldwide Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement had its origin and strongest expression here in Boston. The Deities of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa whom you personally installed and named Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīvallabha are full of this wonderful mood of love and compassion. Most appropriately, your bringing their beloved Lordships Rādhā-Gopīvallabha here is the crowning glory to forever glorify the connection to the love of Vṛndāvana and the compassion of Lord Caitanya and how the mood of the preacher is the same as that of a resident of Vṛndāvana like yourself.

And certainly it was the Lord’s divine arrangement that had the *Jaladuta* bring you to Boston first to express the internal mood of love that is the real meaning of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness. The sweet mood of preaching and serving here is so full of all these wonderful variegated internal moods and feelings that we feel ourselves most blessed and fortunate to serve you and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīvallabha here in Boston. The steady turnover every year of new, young people is always a steady reminder for us to stay close to you and fully absorbed in serving the principle of connecting the material to the spiritual, or *yukta-vairāgya,* for the pleasure of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, following your superexcellent example.

Thus our lives have become so wonderful, for we have been given the most rare and fortunate opportunity to serve you. Śrīla Prabhupāda, here in Boston, where your personal moods of love and compassion in your reciprocation with Rādhā-Gopīvallabha so much permeate and govern the sweet and exclusive mood of devotional service that is available here.

So on this sacred day of your happy divine appearance, let us renew and deepen our awareness and appreciation of that which we are surrounded by at every moment of the whole year: the sweet moods and feelings of love for Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, Rādhā-Gopīvallabha, Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, and Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā, as you lead us and teach us to reciprocate with Them.

You live forever by your divine instructions, and we, your followers, live with you. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīvallabha’s New Guṇḍicā Mandira, Boston, USA.  
(written by Rāma Rāya dāsa)

## New Haridwar, Khasadeśa

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet again and again. All glories to you and your beloved Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya!

A few days ago, when we told a new *bhaktin* that we were going to write a Vyāsa-pūjā offering to you, she was a little surprised, How could it be? Śrīla Prabhupāda left this planet many years ago; how could he read your Vyāsa-pūjā? At the moment we were left without words to reply to her and just laughed. Later, as we pondered her question, trying to give an answer, we realized that actually it is your spiritual presence that not only encourages us through all difficulties on the path of devotion but makes the whole Khasadeśa mission advance in spite of all the obstacles. On the matter of devotional service to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, either in our individual endeavors or in the collective mission in this Age of Kali, you are the final resting place, the instructor, the source of spiritual inspiration, the maintainer, the commander, the real seer and supervisor. You were, you are, and you will always be with us, your followers, spreading the *saṅkīrtana* movement and leading all the fallen souls back home, back to Godhead.

You are the soul of our movement and the soul of our souls. Just as Lord Kṛṣṇa acted as the charioteer for His eternal friend Arjuna and carried Him on the battlefield of Kurukṣetra five thousand years ago, Lord Caitanya is carrying you on the chariot of your books, enabling you to enter into the hearts of all the conditioned souls to win victory over the modern *asuras*.

Six or seven years ago, the seed of devotion was planted in this part of the world at our Haridwar *yātrā.* We were the handful of devotees who first received your causeless mercy here in Khasadeśa. Inspired by your books and your disciples who repeated your words faithfully, we struggled to please you by distributing your books. Although the result was very small, we thought we would deliver your books into the hands of all the people of Khasadeśa.

Then suddenly our preaching work was stopped by the *asuras,* and the devotees scattered to different cities, struggling hard simply to maintain their lives and families. It seemed the Haridwar *yātrā* would be closed forever. But who knows the strength of the desire of a pure Vaiṣṇava? Who knows the mystic plan of Lord Caitanya? During the last four or five years, your books themselves went into the hands of hundreds and thousands people who were practicing other religious disciplines, and those books moved their hearts. Even though they were impersonalists, they began boldly distributing your *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* in large quantities, thus preparing a large field for further preaching. When some of them in New Haridwar met devotees, they rejected their false teachers, who were interpreting your *Bhagavad-gītā* in an impersonal way, and became the servants of your servants. Thus the New Haridwar *yātrā* was being shaped again. So it is you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, through your *Bhagavad-gītā*, who organized this *yātrā*.

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda! You have amazed all of your servants in Khasadeśa. You have again proved your leadership over all spiritualists and your all-pervading spiritual influence among the people who approach you. Was it their so-called guru who made the miracle? Certainly not. You and your books made the miracle. It happened due to your sweet desire and the strength of your books. Your sweet desire is summarized in the final verse of the *Gītā-māhātmya* that you cite in your Introduction:

*Ekaṁ śāstraṁ devakī-putra-gītam:* let there be one scripture only, one common scripture for the whole world—*Bhagavad-gītā*. *Eko devo devakī-putra eva:* let there be one God for the whole world—Śrī Kṛṣṇa. *Eko mantras tasya nāmāni:* and one hymn, one mantra, one prayer—the chanting of His name: Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare. *Karmāpy ekaṁ tasya devasya sevā:* and let there be one work only—the service of the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

What is the special strength of your *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is*? That you answer in the first purport:

One should read *Bhagavad-gītā* very scrutinizingly with the help of a person who is a devotee of Śrī Kṛṣṇa and try to understand it without personally motivated interpretations. The example of clear understanding is there in the *Bhagavad-gītā* itself, in the way the teaching is understood by Arjuna, who heard the *Gītā* directly from the Lord. If someone is fortunate enough to understand *Bhagavad-gītā* in that line of disciplic succession, without motivated interpretation, then he surpasses all studies of Vedic wisdom, and all scriptures of the world. One will find in the *Bhagavad-gītā* all that is contained in other scriptures, but the reader will also find things which are not to be found elsewhere. That is the specific standard of the *Gītā*. It is the perfect theistic science because it is directly spoken by the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Who can resist your sweet desire, which is the same as that of your Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa? Who can resist the spiritual power of your *Bhagavad-gītā,* which is empowered by the Supreme Personality of Godhead, who is its speaker, and the whole disciplic succession coming from Him?

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, finally we understand that in fact it is you all along who have been pushing the whole Khasadeśa *yātrā* and pushing us to advance in Kṛṣṇa consciousness although we thought it was we who carried you to the Khasa people.

Please accept our humble Vyāsa-pūjā offering and forgive our childish misunderstanding. Please give us your blessings as our eternal master and well-wisher so that we can follow in your footsteps without any deviation and give pure Kṛṣṇa consciousness to all the suffering souls in this part of the world.

Recently, the *asuras* of this country again announced war against religion, and ignorant people follow them. The battle is going on. Please continue to command us, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda. You are the most powerful commander of Lord Caitanya. We have no other shelter but your lotus feet. Please lead us, carry us under the dust of your lotus feet, and remove all the obstacles before us, until we obtain victory.

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda!

The servants of your servants in New Haridwar, Khasadeśa.

## New Hastināpura, Potomac, Maryland, USA

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace and to the all-merciful Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Madana-mohana.

Sunrises and sunsets come and go, while this earthly planet rocks back and forth through the yearly cycle of seasonal changes. In the midst of this passage of time, the eternal glorification of the Lord, which you expanded in so many ways, goes on and on and on. On countless tongues and in millions of homes the transcendental vibration “Hare Kṛṣṇa,” transmitted by your heartfelt *kīrtana* of the holy name, continues to be vibrated without cessation. Twenty-four hours a day people worldwide are chanting the holy names of the Lord with great faith. This spiritual phenomenon is a direct result of the tireless preaching you performed in spreading the holy name throughout the world. It was not only chanting, however, that you brought: it was the gift of life.

As a result of being inspired by your teachings and personal example, thousands of people all over the world are performing varieties of services to the Lord with love and devotion in connection with your Kṛṣṇa consciousness society. Many of these are traditional Vaiṣṇava services, and some are innovative services being performed according to the current time and circumstances. By rendering service in these ways, each of us is striving to utilize the gifts we have been given by the Lord’s grace, so that we may offer them to you in *paramparā* and thus embrace a lifetime of favorable devotional service. Many times you asked us to live our lives in that way. As individuals and as a society dedicated to your vision, we are dutybound to respond to your request with utmost good faith. Much depends on the gift of faith. May we be broadminded (how you love that term!) in our reception of your message and in applying your teachings faithfully in our lives.

That is the path you always encouraged us to follow. You wanted us to follow in your footsteps and do as you were doing. To do that, though, you told us we must become empowered in our activities. You said that we would need to be given power of attorney from the spiritual master and Śrī Kṛṣṇa to actually live and preach in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

How can we obtain this vital power of attorney? Generally people grant power of attorney over their affairs to those who have always shown them loyalty, love, and devotion. Of course, your magnanimity is great, and our experience is that from the first day we came to you, you encouraged us to act “as if” we had been granted this well-earned power of attorney. Under your direction, we chanted Hare Kṛṣṇa in the streets, preached, cooked offerings, worshiped the Deity, and engaged in a variety of other activities, all with the sense of being empowered by your grace—and you taught us to see all of these activities as preaching. To preserve this sense of empowerment for the long term, you emphasized that we would need to become and remain empowered in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. “Power of attorney”—What a fine example you chose to help us remember that if we want Kṛṣṇa consciousness, we must retain your trust by our loyalty and service! As you would say, “That is wanted.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, in a 1965 letter to your dear American hostess Sally Agarwal, you wrote about your coming to America: “My mission is not \[to\] turn any one from the affiliation of a particular religion but I want to let them know more knowledge about God and devotion.” As individuals, and as a society of devotees, may we always embrace the spirit of your mission by living faithfully, humbly, and compassionately as your followers, as the Hare Kṛṣṇa people.

Your humble servants at ISKCON’s New Hastināpura-dhāma, Potomac, Maryland, USA.  
(written by Haridāsa dāsa)

## New Jagannātha Purī, Berkeley, California, USA

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace! Unless we take the dust of your lotus feet upon on our heads, we cannot get free from our unwanted habits (*anarthas*).

Anyone who even once chants the holy name of Kṛṣṇa and glorifies Your Divine Grace is a devotee. However, we must endeavor to purify that devotion until it becomes like unalloyed steel. In particular, we must be careful to avoid offending Your Divine Grace. There are many ways that offenses can occur. Of course, you are very loving, compassionate, and forgiving. Nevertheless, we must be attentive to following your instructions. If we disobey your instructions, we are committing an offense (*guru-avajña*). This offense will also be considered by Kṛṣṇa as an offense against the holy name (offense #3). Furthermore, the *bhakti-latā,* the creeper of devotion, will be uprooted and the leaves of the creeper will dry up (*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya* 19.156).

You have given us so many instructions, but you especially want us to chant the *pañca-tattva* mantra and sixteen rounds of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra*. We have to chant them attentively and carefully. We must follow the four regulatives. We must come together and perform *saṅkīrtana*. We must preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We must continue the worship of the Deities. Here in the Berkeley Temple, we feel the special blessings and association of Your Divine Grace. In 1967, on Gaura Pūrṇimā day, Your Divine Grace personally called upon Lord Jagannātha, Lord Baladeva, and Śrīmatī Subhadrā to come down and sit before us as the *arcā-vigraha*. You personally initiated the first Ratha-yātrā in San Francisco. Book distribution began in the San Francisco Bay Area. You are continuing to expand the preaching in the Bay Area that you yourself started, and we only pray that we may become better servants to assist you in your preaching.
